### Decision 2026-07-08-065: ADR ratification batch — ADR-0026 (DigiLocker signature policy), ADR-0027 (push provider), ADR-0028 (WhatsApp provider), ADR-0029 (WA webhook ingress + opt-in lifecycle), ADR-0030 (compensating-audit mechanization)

**Decision type:** Governance — ADR ratification (Trustee Panel consent-sheet session). Source: `docs/knowledge-transfer/adr-ratification-consent-sheet-2026-07-08.md` (reviewed + signed).
**Status:** Trustee-ratified (attested — the consent sheet carries the panel's per-row marks + a signed Session Resolution).
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1) + Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2) — ≥2-trustee quorum met, 2026-07-08.

**Context:**
The 2026-07-08 consent sheet collected trustee ratification for five ADRs that were all `drafted` and un-presented: ADR-0026 (authored 2026-06-25 at Story 3.3a closure), ADR-0027 and ADR-0028 (both authored 2026-07-05, at Stories 5.2 and 5.3 closure respectively), ADR-0029 (authored 2026-07-06 at Story 5.4 closure), and ADR-0030 (authored 2026-07-08 at Epic 5 retrospective AI-5-3 closure, revised through two pre-ratification amendment passes the same day). The panel signed all five rows "Ratify," with no amendments or conditions recorded on any row.

Preparing the sheet surfaced an **index-hygiene gap**, not a ratification-status problem: `docs/knowledge-transfer/adr-index.md` carried a Section A row for ADR-0026 only. ADR-0027, ADR-0028, and ADR-0029 had each been authored and correctly landed `drafted` in `docs/adr/`, but no index row was ever added for them. Per [[feedback_record_unattested_no_backfill]], this entry does not fabricate an earlier index presence for those three — it records their true authoring dates in the ADR files' own changelogs and adds the index row now, ratified in the same session it is first indexed (mirroring the ADR-0025 precedent for a slot that reaches the index and `ratified` in one event).

**Decision:**

1. **ADR-0026-digilocker-signature-policy — RATIFIED.** Two-window certificate staleness budget: fresh ≤7 days (trusted, no alarm), within-budget 7–30 days (trusted + ops alarm), hard limit >30 days (new KYC verifications fail closed → `pending-valid` manual trustee fallback; existing verified members unaffected); annual trust-anchor/KEK review + daily certificate refresh; key-compromise re-verification queue. Flagged **trustee-judgment** weight on the consent sheet (P0 KYC security policy) — read and ratified as drafted, no amendment.
2. **ADR-0027-push-provider-selection — RATIFIED.** A single `firebase-admin` SDK sends to both Android (FCM HTTP v1) and iOS (APNs) — no native APNs `.p8` credential path, no second auth-refresh mechanism. Light-touch vendor selection behind the Story 5.1 provider-stub abstraction.
3. **ADR-0028-whatsapp-provider-selection — RATIFIED.** The `whatsapp` channel integrates directly with Meta's WhatsApp Business Cloud API for v1 — no BSP intermediary (Gupshup/Twilio/WATI). Light-touch; AR-53 keeps a future BSP swap a single-module change.
4. **ADR-0029-whatsapp-webhook-ingress-and-opt-in-lifecycle — RATIFIED.** Per-Pariwar signed webhook URL (`X-Hub-Signature-256` keyed by the Pariwar's app secret, verification key resolved from the path before the body is trusted); persist-and-ack + async pg-boss worker drain; opt-in lifecycle split across a dedicated state table + the Story 2.7 consent registry. Flagged **policy-adjacent** on the consent sheet for the AR-16 user-initiated-only consent posture — read closely by the panel; no amendment resulted.
5. **ADR-0030-compensating-audit-mechanization — RATIFIED.** Shared `withCompensatingAudit` helper (`packages/domain/src/audit`) closing 5 confirmed compensating-audit gaps in `channel-config`/`degraded-mode`, backfilling 4 existing hand-rolled implementations, and an AST gate enforcing the positive "reaches `withCompensatingAudit`" invariant (gated on rollback-capable transaction-handle presence, not mutation-name heuristics). Light-touch — same family as the already-ratified ADR-0012–0015 CI-gate cluster. Two-commit implementation sequencing recorded in the ADR (§5): Commit 1 — helper + five-site fix; Commit 2 — four-module backfill + AST gate.

**Cascade applied:**
1. `docs/adr/ADR-0026-digilocker-signature-policy.md`, `ADR-0027-push-provider-selection.md`, `ADR-0028-whatsapp-provider-selection.md`, `ADR-0029-whatsapp-webhook-ingress-and-opt-in-lifecycle.md`, `ADR-0030-compensating-audit-mechanization.md` — each `Status: drafted → ratified`, `Date` set to 2026-07-08, `Ratifying trustees` filled in, changelog row appended.
2. `docs/knowledge-transfer/adr-index.md` — ADR-0026 and ADR-0030 rows flipped `drafted` → `ratified`; ADR-0027/0028/0029 added as new Section A rows (the index-hygiene gap) and ratified in the same edit; status-count table corrected and updated (`drafted` 1→0 net, `ratified` 23→28, Total 143→147 — the total also absorbs a same-day bookkeeping correction for the ADR-0030 row added earlier 2026-07-08 without being posted to the count); reconciliation footnote appended per `README.md` §7 ledger-vs-component reconciliation discipline.
3. `.decision-log.md` — this entry.

**Open follow-ups:**
- Implementation of ADR-0030 proceeds per its two-commit sequencing (§5): Commit 1 (helper + five-site fix), then Commit 2 (four-module backfill + AST gate).
- None of the other four ratified ADRs (0026–0029) carry an open follow-up beyond what their own Consequences sections already name (e.g. ADR-0026's quarterly key-compromise-procedure rehearsal, already an existing operational commitment, not new).

**References:**
- `docs/knowledge-transfer/adr-ratification-consent-sheet-2026-07-08.md` (the signed sheet + Session Resolution — the attestation)
- `docs/adr/ADR-0026-digilocker-signature-policy.md` · `docs/adr/ADR-0027-push-provider-selection.md` · `docs/adr/ADR-0028-whatsapp-provider-selection.md` · `docs/adr/ADR-0029-whatsapp-webhook-ingress-and-opt-in-lifecycle.md` · `docs/adr/ADR-0030-compensating-audit-mechanization.md`
- `docs/knowledge-transfer/adr-index.md` Section A (rows updated/added this session)
- `epic-5-retro-2026-07-08.md` §7 AI-5-3 (ADR-0030's originating action item)
- Memory: [[feedback_record_unattested_no_backfill]] (the ADR-0027/0028/0029 index-add is a hygiene-gap correction, not a fabricated earlier index presence) + [[feedback_closure_language_precision]] (ratified vs. drafted precision maintained per-row)

### Decision 2026-07-05-064: Trustee consent session — Phase-0 framework ratifications R1-R4 (legal-counsel review scope, KT pack, degradation policy, launch-gate inventory); R5 excluded as already-closed

**Decision type:** Governance — trustee consent-sheet session (Story 0.13 Task 7 + Story 0.5 Task 8 + Story 0.4 Task 7 + Story 0.15 Task 8 ratifications). Source: `docs/knowledge-transfer/trustee-consent-sheet-phase0-framework-ratifications.md` (reviewed + signed).
**Status:** Trustee-ratified (attested — the consent sheet carries the panel's per-row marks + a signed Session Resolution).
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1) + Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2) — ≥2-trustee quorum met, 2026-07-05.

**Context:**
The 2026-07-05 consent sheet collected trustee ratification for the **READY** cluster (R1-R5) of `docs/knowledge-transfer/outstanding-trustee-actions.md`. The panel signed all five rows on the sheet ("Ratified/signed: all"). While running the per-row cascade, R5 (Story 0.14 Task 7 — native-stack experiment scope + device-procurement budget) was found to already be **closed since 2026-06-05** via Decision 2026-06-05-030 (the Q14.1-Q14.4 trustee-questionnaire mechanism) — a fact neither the outstanding-trustee-actions register nor the consent sheet reflected (both carried it forward as still-pending R5). Per [[feedback_record_unattested_no_backfill]], this entry does **not** record a second ratification of an already-ratified item; it records R1-R4 and corrects the R5 staleness for the trustees' awareness.

**Decision:**

**R1 — Legal-counsel review scope ratified.** `docs/legal-counsel-engagement/review-scope-charter.md` (the 5 AC-named scope items + 32-row cross-Story deferred-scope inventory + 13-row regulatory-surface review + 6-row pre-launch checkpoint coverage) ratified pack-as-a-unit. Discharges Story 0.13 **Task 7**. Recorded in `legal-counsel-engagement/engagement-ledger.md` §3. **Unblocks B1** — counsel shortlist + selection (Task 8) may now begin; this is the critical-path item per the register.

**R2 — Knowledge-Transfer pack ≥2-trustee sign-off.** `docs/knowledge-transfer/` components (ADR-index, Niyamavali→FR mapping, deployment-topology, on-call-playbook, third-party-dependency-inventory) + comprehension questionnaire + answer key, signed pack-as-a-unit. Discharges Story 0.5 **Task 8**. Recorded in `kt-pack-ledger.md` §2 (two trustee rows, quorum met); framework-commit row status superseded `Author-committed; awaiting trustee sign-off` → `Trustee-ratified`. Task 9 (comprehension-questionnaire administration to the backup engineer) stays open, gated on Story 0.6 (B3).

**R3 — Degradation policy ≥2-trustee sign-off (framework; comms-templates carve-out).** `docs/degradation-policy/` surface-inventory + table-top-exercise runbook, signed per-bundle. Discharges Story 0.4 **Task 7 for the framework only**. Recorded in `degradation-policy-ledger.md` Trustee sign-off log (two rows). Per closure-language precision, this is **not** a full AC-1 closure: the 5 comms templates carry `PENDING LEGAL REVIEW` and re-sign individually once Story 0.13 counsel returns (B1) — recorded as still-open, not asserted closed.

**R4 — Architectural launch-gate inventory ratified + monthly-review cadence armed.** `docs/launch-gate-inventory/inventory-roster.md` (15-row roster) + `monthly-review-cadence-protocol.md` ratified as the tracker + review process. Discharges Story 0.15 **Task 8** and arms **Task 9** (first monthly review, now due). Recorded in `launch-gate-inventory/engagement-ledger.md` §3. This ratifies the *tracker*, not the individual rows — per-row status is unchanged. **Roster Rows 1+2** (already `in-progress` pending this exact ratification per Decision 2026-06-03-015 D-01) may now be evaluated for closure at the first monthly review.

**Slip recorded (D-03 discipline, `engagement-ledger.md` §3):** Task 8 ratification was due on or before **2026-07-01** (Decision 2026-06-03-015 D-03, 4 weeks from Story 0.15 Task 7 author-commit). It occurred **2026-07-05** — a **4-day slip**. Slip reason: session-scheduling delay; no roster row was affected by the delay and no ratified content changed as a result. Per `monthly-review-cadence-protocol.md` §3, this is surfaced as the first agenda item of the now-convened first monthly review rather than silently absorbed.

**R5 — EXCLUDED; correction recorded, not a new ratification.** Story 0.14 Task 7 (native-stack experiment scope + device-procurement budget) was ratified **2026-06-05** via Decision 2026-06-05-030 (Q14.1-Q14.4), a full month before this session. At that same session Task 8 (device procurement) and Task 9 Days 1-9 (prototype build) also closed — materially further along than either `outstanding-trustee-actions.md` (as-of 2026-06-24) or today's consent sheet reflected. **No new ratification is recorded for R5 here.** The genuinely open Story 0.14 items are Task 10 (Solo Builder measurement collection — not yet run) and Task 11 (ratify-or-pivot decision + ≥1-trustee acknowledgement, per UX spec line 845) — tracked as B5, unchanged by this session.

**Cascade applied:**

2. `docs/knowledge-transfer/kt-pack-ledger.md` §2 + §1 — R2 sign-off rows + framework-commit status supersession.

4. `docs/launch-gate-inventory/engagement-ledger.md` §3 — R4 ratification row + slip note.

6. `docs/knowledge-transfer/outstanding-trustee-actions.md` — R1-R4 moved to "recently cleared"; R5 entry corrected to reflect its true 2026-06-05 closure; B1 marked newly unblocked; as-of date refreshed.
7. `.decision-log.md` — this entry.

- **[GATE] Story 0.5 Task 9** — comprehension-questionnaire administration, gated on Story 0.6 backup-engineer onboarding (B3).

### Decision 2026-06-24-063: Retire the AI-cadence instrument (instrumentation-only) — re-attestation obligation released; 1.497 finding stands

**Decision type:** Other — governance instrument retirement
**Status:** Trustee-ratified
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul + Kalpana Bharti (same panel as Decisions 2026-06-24-061/062)

**Context:**
The AI-cadence instrument (`ai-cadence-actuals.md` + the `cadence-check` CI gate), stood up per Epic 1 retrospective AI-1 to instrument the 80 hr/wk NET assumption behind `ceiling_ratio = 1.497` (which cleared the `max(floor_ratio, ceiling_ratio) > 1.5` trigger by 0.003), was found at the Epic 2 retrospective to be perpetually un-populated: the gate asserts file existence + currency, NOT row population. Root cause — the instrument proved non-capturable in a solo + AI workflow (session wall-time ≠ net output; idle/context-switch time not cleanly separable), so the re-attestation obligation cannot be satisfied with trustworthy data. Drives Epic 2 retrospective AI-2-1.

**Decision:**
1. Retire the AI-cadence instrument — instrumentation only: remove the `cadence-check` gate (`scripts/cadence-check/check.ts`, the `cadence:check` script, the `ci.yml` job, the `ci-local.sh` registration) and archive `ai-cadence-actuals.md` as RETIRED in place.
2. Release the per-epic re-attestation obligation (it cannot be satisfied with trustworthy data in this workflow).
3. No backfill — Epic 0/1/2 remain recorded UN-ATTESTED (record-un-attested-no-backfill discipline).
4. The Epic-0 `ceiling_ratio = 1.497` no-trigger finding (Decision 2026-06-04-016) STANDS as ratified; `reconciliation-decision-framework.md §1` and its 1.5× trigger are unchanged. Instrumentation-only retirement.
Recorded in ADR-0025-retire-ai-cadence-instrument (`ratified`).

**Open follow-ups:**
- None on this decision. Related Epic 2 retrospective items AI-2-2 (domain-accessor-invariant gate) and AI-2-3 (test-quality checkpoint) are tracked in `epic-2-retro-2026-06-24.md`.

**References:**
- `docs/adr/ADR-0025-retire-ai-cadence-instrument.md`
- `.decision-log.md` Decision 2026-06-04-016 (80 hr/wk cadence sign-off)
- `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/epic-1-retro-2026-06-20.md` (AI-1) · `epic-2-retro-2026-06-24.md` (AI-2-1, H-1) · `ai-cadence-actuals.md` (archived RETIRED)
- `docs/spec-to-cadence-reconciliation/reconciliation-decision-framework.md §1` (unchanged)

### Decision 2026-06-24-062: ADR ratification — ADR-0022 ratified at the 2026-06-24 Trustee Panel session + three in-session governance amendments

**Decision type:** ADR ratification (architectural) + governance amendments — ratifies ADR-0022 (public Astro SSR shell + cache-safe public render) with three trustee-directed amendments incorporated into the ADR.
**Status:** Trustee-ratified (attested — trustee marks and amendment directives received 2026-06-24).
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1) + Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2) — ≥2-trustee quorum met.

**Context:**
ADR-0022 was authored at Story 2.5 closure (2026-06-21) and remained `drafted` — it was noted in Decision 2026-06-21-059 as "authored but not presented." The Trustee Panel ratified it at the 2026-06-24 session (same session as Decision 061) and directed three amendments before the cascade was applied. All three amendments are incorporated directly into the ADR file; no open follow-ups were created.

**Decision:**

ADR-0022 (`public-astro-ssr-shell-cache-safety`) **ratified**, prior status `drafted`, weight trustee-judgment (first public unauthenticated surface; the architecture's mandatory "committed in an ADR" cache-safety artifact).

**Governance amendments adopted in-session:**

- **A. Accepted Risk section.** An explicit `## Accepted Risk` section is added to the ADR formalising the direct-DB-read risk: four structural mitigations enumerated (role+RLS, public-tier-only, write-credential prohibition, PII scrape gate); Epic 11b revisit trigger recorded. The risk was previously mentioned in Consequences/Negative; it is now a named, structured section.
- **B. Write-capable credential prohibition.** §3 (Unauthenticated read-scope) now explicitly states: write-capable credentials must not be issued to or configured in `apps/public`; the database role must carry `SELECT`-only grants. This is a provisioning constraint, not merely code convention.
- **C. Cache key completeness rule.** §2 (Cache-safe public SSR guarantee) now includes an explicit rule: cache keys must include all language selection inputs (`Accept-Language` header + `?lang=` query parameter override). Failure to do so constitutes cache poisoning via locale variation; `Vary: Accept-Language` + distinct `?lang=` URLs are the enforcement mechanism.

**Cascade applied (3 surfaces):**
1. **ADR-0022 file** — `Status → ratified`, `Ratifying trustees` populated, three amendments applied in-ADR, `## Ratification (2026-06-24)` section appended summarising the amendments.
2. **`adr-index.md`** — ADR-0022 Section A row flipped `drafted → ratified` with this decision cross-linked and the 3 amendments noted in the cell; breakdown updated (`drafted` 1→0, `ratified` 21→22); line-17 ledger note updated. **All 22 Section-A ADRs are now `ratified`; no `drafted` rows remain.**
3. **`.decision-log.md`** — this entry.

**Open follow-ups:**
- **Epic 11b** — evaluate whether to adopt the thin-public-API data-path alternative (keeps DB creds out of the public renderer). Not a trustee gate; BigDev judgment call.

### Decision 2026-06-23-060: Trustee consent session — 9 runbook authority sign-offs + empty/skeleton/error inventory ratification + escrow sealing authorization + a deploy/rollback execution-validation directive

**Decision type:** Governance — trustee consent-sheet session (runbook authority + artifact ratification + escrow authorization + execution-validation directive). Source: `docs/knowledge-transfer/trustee-consent-sheet-2026-06-23.md` (reviewed + signed).

**Context:**
The 2026-06-23 consent sheet collected trustee consent for the signature-blocked governance backlog. The panel signed every row. This entry records the session and runs the 3-surface cascade per the sheet's "what I do per signed row".

**Decision:**

**Group A — runbook authority: ≥2-trustee sign-off (9 runbooks).** Signed against git SHA `f247e6d`; recorded in `docs/runbooks/operational-readiness-ledger.md` sign-off table; each runbook header flipped `draft → signed-off`:
`deploy.md`, `rollback.md`, `multi-pariwar-provisioning.md` (the three AS-BUILT Story-1.15 reconciliations), `secret-rotation.md`, `audit-log-integrity-verification.md`, `reconciliation-manual-intervention.md`, `rbac-seed-reset.md`, `job-queue-operations.md`, `trustee-credential-loss-succession.md`.
- Signing `deploy.md` + `rollback.md` + `multi-pariwar-provisioning.md` **discharges the ADR-0011 co-requisite** (Decision 2026-06-21-050) — the 3 reconciled runbooks are now ≥2-trustee re-signed.
- Signing `trustee-credential-loss-succession.md` **discharges the Decision 2026-06-21-059 amendment-A** "succession runbook before production go-live" gate (the document gate; the execution-validation directive below is a separate, still-open gate).

**Group B — governance artifact ratification.** `docs/ux/empty-skeleton-error-inventory.md` (Story 2.5 `apps/public` surfaces) **≥2-trustee ratified** — this **attests** the ratification leg recorded **un-attested-pending** in Decision 2026-06-21-058 (supersedes that leg). Launch-gate **Row 6 stays `in-progress`** (the `closed` trigger remains the full Phase-1 surface inventory at Epic 11a; closure criteria NOT relaxed).

**Group C — credential escrow: sealing authorization.** The panel **authorized** the Story 0.2 Task-7 sealing of the three `sealable-now` `prod-credential` envelopes (`cloudflare-account-admin`, `dokploy-substrate-admin`, `trust-bank-operational-access`) + the Task-8 dry-run (≥1 per envelope-class), under the ratified ADR-0001 mechanism + custodial paths. **Execution-pending:** the physical seal events are recorded in `escrow-ledger.md` when performed; until then the seals are **un-attested** (not fabricated).

**Trustee directive — deploy/rollback execution-validation (NEW gated requirement).** Beyond the document sign-off, the panel directs that **`deploy.md` and `rollback.md` each have at least one successful, documented execution using the now-signed runbooks** before production go-live — ideally by a non-Solo-Builder engineer under simulated bus-factor activation (the Story 0.1 AC-4 model). A gap found in execution re-opens the corresponding runbook's sign-off via the AC-3 fix → re-sign → re-execute protocol.

**Discharges:** ADR-0011 co-requisite (Decision 2026-06-21-050) · Decision 2026-06-21-059 amendment-A document gate · the un-attested-pending ratification leg of Decision 2026-06-21-058.

**Open follow-ups (gated — do NOT assert as closed):**

**Governance amendments adopted in-session (the canonical record; the ADR files carry pointers here):**

- **A. Super Admin governance model (amends ADR-0009 admin-authentication).** (1) Super Admin manages ordinary admin accounts. (2) Super Admin assignment requires trustee approval. (3) At least one active trustee must continuously hold Super Admin access. (4) No single non-trustee individual may be the sole holder of Super Admin privileges. **Follow-up (gated):** a trustee credential-loss & succession runbook is **required before production go-live**, covering lost passkeys, exhausted recovery codes, trustee death / resignation / incapacity, and Super Admin transfer/revocation procedures.
- **B. OQ-3 resolved (amends ADR-0008 rbac-permission-model).** (1) The 12-role catalogue is approved. (2) The seed permission matrix is approved **subject to implementation of the trustee-approved amendments**. (3) Future permission additions for Finance Officer, Media/Comms, Field Worker, and Helpline Operator are to be ratified when their feature sets ship. The ADR-0008 "provisional pending OQ-3" qualifier is now resolved-with-conditions (no longer open).
- **C. Niyamavali workflow governance clarification (ADR-0021).** The audit-logged draft→publish workflow is the **authoritative path** by which Niyamavali amendments become official and publishable within the platform.

**Cascade applied (3 surfaces, per the consent-sheet "what I do per ratified row"):**
1. **ADR files** — each of the 18 files: `Status → ratified`, `Date 2026-06-21`, `Ratifying trustees` populated, changelog row appended where a changelog table exists; ADR-0008 OQ-3 consequence annotated resolved; ADR-0009 gains a Ratification section carrying amendment A.

Per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]] the drift correction is recorded, not silently absorbed.
3. **`.decision-log.md`** — this entry.

### Decision 2026-06-21-058: P0-4 Empty/Skeleton/Error Inventory — Story 2.5 `apps/public` surfaces authored + author-committed; ≥2-trustee ratification recorded un-attested-pending

**Decision type:** Launch-gate artifact (UX deliverable) — discharges the re-homed P0-4 architectural gate (`inventory-roster.md` Row 6) for the Story 2.5 surface set. Re-home origin: Decision 2026-06-20-054 (Epic-1-retro AI-3).
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed. **≥2-trustee ratification: un-attested-pending** (see Open follow-ups).
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)

**Context:**
Architectural P0-4 = "Empty/Skeleton/Error Inventory" (architecture L4783) was committed to Story 1.17 (Epic-0 A-4) but Story 1.17 shipped without it. Decision 2026-06-20-054 (AI-3) RE-HOMED it to **Story 2.5** — the first public surface (`apps/public` Astro SSR shell) — with the binding that the inventory cover every `apps/public` screen surface built at 2.5. Story 2.5 has now landed those surfaces (the public Niyamavali list + version/diff sub-views + the shell's 404/500 routes).

**Decision:**
1. **Inventory authored + author-committed.** `docs/ux/empty-skeleton-error-inventory.md` enumerates the empty / skeleton / error state for **all 5 Story-2.5 `apps/public` surfaces** (Niyamavali list, version-history sub-view, diff sub-view, 404, 500). **No `<TBD>` cells** for the 2.5 surfaces; every `N/A` carries a recorded rationale.
2. **Skeleton/loading recorded N/A by design.** Every `apps/public` route is fully server-rendered with no client fetch and no hydration (`js_bundle_bytes: 0`), so there is no client loading phase to skeleton. This is recorded with rationale, not left blank — a faithful "N/A", not a gap.
3. **Inventory is PARTIAL at 2.5.** It covers `apps/public` only; it is extended at Epic 11a (Member Directory) and Epic 11b (per-claim + In Memoriam). `inventory-roster.md` Row 6 flips `open` → **`in-progress`** (NOT `closed`) — closure is reserved for the full Phase-1 surface inventory at Epic 11a completion (Row 6 closure_criteria preserved verbatim).
4. **≥2-trustee ratification recorded UN-ATTESTED-PENDING.** Per [[feedback_record_unattested_no_backfill]], the dev workflow authors + author-commits the artifact; it does NOT reconstruct a Trustee-Panel ratification event that did not occur in-session. The ≥2-trustee ratification leg is carried openly as a **gated open follow-up** below — integrity over appearance. This is honest about the closure state: artifact = Closed by [edit]; trustee ratification = Not yet attested.

**Open follow-ups:**
- **[GATE] ≥2-trustee ratification of this inventory** (the Row 6 closure_criteria signature). Convene the Trustee Panel (Dhiraj Rahul + Kalpana Bharti per the Decision 2026-06-21-057 panel) to ratify `docs/ux/empty-skeleton-error-inventory.md`; on ratification, append a successor decision recording the attested ratification and (at Epic 11a full-surface completion) flip Row 6 `in-progress` → `closed`. Until then the trustee leg remains un-attested — do NOT assert it as complete.

### Decision 2026-06-21-057: ADR-0010 ratified — Cloudflare edge/WAF DPDPA legal review cleared ("acceptable as designed") + ≥2-trustee ratification

**Decision type:** ADR ratification (architectural) — Edge/WAF provider commitment; counsel-co-signed per Decision 2026-06-05-034 §5. Resolves launch-gate Row 3 ([P0] Edge/WAF DPDPA gate) + Decision 2026-06-20-051 open follow-ups #1–#2.
**Status:** Trustee-ratified.
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1) + Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2)
**Legal counsel (DPDPA clearance):** Adv. Mohit Agrawal (Story 0.13 engaged counsel)

**Context:**
ADR-0010 (Cloudflare as v1 edge/WAF default; `packages/edge` pivot abstraction; Turnstile server-verify + AC-3 enforce-result fix; edge-only-ingress mechanism) was authored at Story 1.13 closure and held in `drafted` status with a single OPEN [P0] surface: Cloudflare-DPDPA compatibility pending legal review (architecture L247-254; ADR-0010 §6). Decision 2026-06-20-051 moved that review off Epic 2's critical path and recorded a flip to `under-trustee-review` as pending the legal-review evidence (its open follow-ups #1–#2). That evidence has now arrived: Story 0.13 engaged counsel Adv. Mohit Agrawal reviewed the design as recorded in ADR-0010 and the Trustee Panel convened on it.

**Decision:**
1. **DPDPA legal review CLEARED.** Adv. Mohit Agrawal returned **"Cloudflare acceptable as designed"** — clearing Cloudflare's data-residency / sub-processor posture for the `asia-south1` target. Per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]] the clearance is scoped to **the edge design as recorded in ADR-0010**; a material change to the edge data-flow (a new sub-processor, a newly-proxied PII surface) re-opens the review.
2. **ADR-0010 ratified.** Status flipped `drafted` → `under-trustee-review` → `ratified` (2026-06-21) by ≥2 trustees (Dhiraj Rahul + Kalpana Bharti). Cloudflare is the committed, operationally-binding v1 edge/WAF provider. §6 flipped OPEN → CLEARED. The `packages/edge` abstraction + §Alternatives pivot path remain the recorded reversibility surface.
3. **D1-1.13 gate NARROWED, not fully closed.** The live `terraform apply` was gated on BOTH (a) legal-review clearance AND (b) a provisioned live Cloudflare zone + account. This decision clears the legal half; the **operational provisioning half remains gated** (deferred-work D1-1.13 / D2-1.13). No live zone is asserted by this decision.
4. **Launch-gate Row 3 closed.** The [P0] Edge/WAF DPDPA-compatibility *decision* gate (`inventory-roster.md` Row 3) flips `open` → `closed` — its closure criteria (counsel return + this `.decision-log.md` ratification of Cloudflare-keep + substantive ADR) are met. The provisioning execution item is tracked separately under D1-1.13, not as a launch-gate decision row.
5. **DPDPA assertion scope.** DPDPA posture may now be asserted for the edge design as recorded in ADR-0010. Do NOT extend the assertion to data-flows beyond that recorded design, nor to surfaces not yet Cloudflare-proxied — Epic 2 surfaces still route via Dokploy per Decision 2026-06-20-051 until the zone is live.

**Open follow-ups:**
- D1-1.13 / D2-1.13 operational provisioning leg (live Cloudflare zone + account; `terraform apply`; push Turnstile secrets to Secret Manager; set `TURNSTILE_SECRET_NAME` + `VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY`; origin-side edge-auth guard). On activation, add a successor decision recording the live-zone posture switch (Decision 2026-06-20-051 follow-up #4).
- Bot-Management plan-tier purchase (ADR-0010 §5 / D5-1.13) remains an ops/contract decision, unaffected by this clearance.
- If the edge data-flow materially changes, re-open the DPDPA review with Adv. Mohit Agrawal.

**References:**
- `docs/adr/ADR-0010-edge-waf-cloudflare-turnstile.md` (ratified; §6 CLEARED; changelog 2026-06-21)
- `docs/knowledge-transfer/adr-index.md` (ADR-0010 row `drafted` → `ratified`; status counts updated `drafted` 18→17 / `ratified` 0→1)

### Decision 2026-06-20-056: AI-8 — Test runbook hardening: H-4 live-DB gotchas codified

**Decision type:** Test infrastructure governance / Epic 1 retrospective AI-8.
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed.
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** n/a (engineering process)

**Context:**
Epic 1 H-4 surfaced four test-infrastructure landmines: (1) 42P07 — regenerating an applied migration causes `drizzle-kit generate` to silently re-use the same `when` timestamp and the journal skips it; (2) 42P01 — resetting via `DROP SCHEMA public` strips `twt_app` role's `USAGE` grant; (3) parallelism flake — own-committing integration tests accumulate rows across concurrent runs; (4) `apps/api` CI filter gap — the integration-tests ci.yml job did not include `--filter=@twt/api`, causing the Story 1.11b trustee-UI endpoint suite to run locally but be invisible to cloud CI. These are recorded in memory `[[project_live_db_test_gotchas]]` but not in a durable project artifact; they will recur if the memory is lost.

**Decision:**
Create `docs/runbooks/test-runbook.md` codifying the H-4 lessons as standing rules for every integration test author. Filed under `docs/runbooks/` (the existing runbook home) to make it discoverable by any engineer without needing memory access.

**Open follow-ups:**
- At each new integration test PR: author confirms they have read the test runbook before merge.
- If a new H-4-class bug surfaces: amend the runbook on the same PR that fixes it.

**References:**
- `docs/runbooks/test-runbook.md` — the runbook artifact (AI-8)
- Memory `[[project_live_db_test_gotchas]]`
- Epic 1 retrospective H-4 + AI-8 — `epic-1-retro-2026-06-20.md`

### Decision 2026-06-20-054: A-4 re-homed to Story 2.5 — Empty/Skeleton/Error inventory formally attached (AI-3)

**Decision type:** Cross-story scope / Epic 1 retrospective AI-3 re-homing.
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed; trustee acknowledgement recommended (affects Story 2.5 scope and inventory-roster.md Row 6 closure path).
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** pending

**Context:**
Epic 0 committed (A-4) to fold the architecture P0-4 Empty/Skeleton/Error inventory into Story 1.17's ACs. That never happened — Story 1.17 shipped without the amendment. The architecture P0-4 gate (inventory-roster.md Row 6) has no owning story and no discharge path. Leaving it orphaned means it remains open indefinitely with no concrete closure trigger. Story 2.5 (`apps/public` Astro SSR shell — Epic 2's first public surface) is the natural home: empty/skeleton/error states are a surface-level UX deliverable and the public Astro shell is the first Phase-1 screen surface where they materially matter.

**Decision:**
1. **A-4 (Empty/Skeleton/Error inventory) is formally attached to Story 2.5.** Story 2.5's Acceptance Criteria are amended to include a UX-led empty/skeleton/error-state inventory covering every screen surface built in `apps/public` at Story 2.5. The inventory artifact lives at `docs/ux/empty-skeleton-error-inventory.md` (or a named equivalent).
2. **Architecture P0-4 gate (inventory-roster.md Row 6) discharge path is now `Story 2.5` + `≥2-trustee ratification`.** Row 6 `cross_story_discharge_path` is updated accordingly.
3. **The micro-story option is NOT taken** — attaching directly to Story 2.5 avoids creating a micro-story that would also need its own sprint-status entry and could itself become an un-gated commitment.
4. **Scope caveat**: Story 2.5's initial public surface is limited (`apps/public` Niyamavali + shell). The inventory covers what 2.5 ships; it is extended at Epic 11a (Member Directory + matrix) and Epic 11b (per-claim + In Memoriam). The Row 6 closure criteria require the inventory to cover "every Phase-1 screen surface" — the 2.5 discharge is partial; the gate flips `in-progress` at 2.5 and `closed` when Epic 11a/11b complete the inventory.

### Decision 2026-06-20-053: Meta-fix — no un-gated commitment rule (AI-2)

**Decision type:** Cross-project governance process / Epic 1 retrospective AI-2.
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed; trustee acknowledgement recommended.
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** pending

**Context:**
Epic 1 retrospective surface the empirical pattern: every *gated* commitment in Epic 0 closed (A-1, A-2); every *un-gated process* commitment slipped (A-4, A-5). A-4 (Empty/Skeleton/Error inventory) and A-5 (ai-cadence-actuals.md) were both process commitments with no enforcing artifact. Both decayed — not through carelessness but because nothing mechanical was watching them. The fix is not "try harder to remember"; it is "give it a gate." This decision records that rule as a durable commitment so it doesn't itself decay.

**Decision:**
1. **Every retro action item must be assigned an enforcing artifact before it is considered "tracked."** Acceptable forms: a CI gate (script + ci.yml job), a file whose existence is CI-checked, a decision-log entry with a named trigger condition, or a story AC that gates the story's done status.
2. **Action items with no enforcing artifact at the time of the retro must be escalated immediately** (before the next sprint starts) to one of the above forms. "We will remember to do it" is not an acceptable gate.
3. **The sprint-status.yaml ledger entry for each story must cite which AI-N items it closes or advances**, so the retro can check follow-through without relying on memory.
4. **The meta-fix itself is gated**: this decision entry is the enforcement artifact for AI-2. Future retros must check this decision entry and verify each new action item has an enforcing artifact before closing the retro.

**Open follow-ups:**
- At each future retrospective: audit new action items for gate coverage before closing the session.
- At Epic 2 retrospective: verify AI-1 through AI-9 from Epic 1 retro all have enforcing artifacts or explicit deferral rationale; cite this decision.

**References:**
- Epic 1 retrospective I-1 + AI-2 — `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/epic-1-retro-2026-06-20.md`
- A-4 (orphaned Empty/Skeleton/Error inventory) + A-5 (un-attested cadence) — the empirical basis
- AI-1 (`ai-cadence-actuals.md` + `cadence-check` CI job) — the enforcement artifact for A-5/AI-1
- AI-9 (`gate-inventory.md`) — shows enforcement-floor status at Epic 2 start

### Decision 2026-06-20-051: Epic 2 public-surface posture — Cloudflare-DPDPA off critical path; Story 2.5 designed non-edge-capable (Dokploy precedent)

**Decision type:** Cross-epic governance / Epic 1 retrospective AI-6 posture gate — pre-kickoff decision before Epic 2 first public surface.
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed; trustee sign-off recommended (this gates a public surface and affects the DPDPA legal-review scope).
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** pending

**Context:**
Epic 2 ships `apps/public` at Story 2.5 — the first publicly reachable surface in the product. ADR-0010 (Cloudflare as v1 edge/WAF default) remains in `drafted` status with the Cloudflare-DPDPA compatibility review still OPEN (architecture L247-254). The live `terraform apply` for the Cloudflare zone is gated on that legal-review clearance (D1-1.13). Allowing the legal review to sit on Epic 2's critical path would make Story 2.5 (and therefore the entire public trust identity milestone) dependent on counsel turnaround time. This decision resolves the posture so Story 2.5 can be designed and launched without that dependency.

The precedent is Story 1.15: `/p/<id>/` traffic routes straight to Dokploy today — Cloudflare path-routing was explicitly NOT enabled because D1-1.13 was unresolved. Story 2.5 inherits the same posture.

**Decision:**
1. **Continue the ADR-0010 legal review off the critical path.** The Cloudflare-DPDPA review proceeds in parallel with Epic 2 development; it does not gate any Epic 2 story.
2. **Story 2.5 (`apps/public` Astro SSR shell) is designed and launched via the Dokploy non-edge posture**, identical to the Story 1.15 precedent. `apps/public` traffic routes straight to Dokploy; Cloudflare path-routing is NOT enabled for `apps/public` until D1-1.13 clears.
3. **Switch to Cloudflare only after legal sign-off.** Once the DPDPA review clears and D1-1.13 is activated (zone provisioned, `terraform apply`), the `packages/edge` abstraction (AR-52) makes the pivot a single-module change — no Story 2.5 rework required.
4. **Do NOT assert DPDPA compliance for any Epic 2 surface.** Per ADR-0010 §6 and deferred-work D1-1.13: the live zone + DPDPA assertion remain gated on the legal-review clearance. Epic 2 assets are served via Dokploy and are not Cloudflare-proxied; DPDPA posture for these surfaces is not asserted until the zone is live.

**Open follow-ups:**
- ADR-0010 trustee ratification remains pending (status still `drafted`); flip to `under-trustee-review` once the legal-review engagement is active and evidence is presented.
- Legal review to continue under Story 0.13 engagement; outcome determines D1-1.13 activation.
- Story 2.5 acceptance criteria must NOT include Cloudflare zone activation as a required AC.
- At D1-1.13 activation: add a successor decision entry recording the posture switch.

**References:**
- ADR-0010 §6 (DPDPA OPEN, D1-1.13 gated) — `docs/adr/ADR-0010-edge-waf-cloudflare-turnstile.md`
- Decision 2026-06-15-050 (Story 1.15 Dokploy precedent — Cloudflare NOT enabled)
- Deferred-work D1-1.13 (live `terraform apply` stay gated) — `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md`
- Epic 1 retrospective AI-6 — `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/epic-1-retro-2026-06-20.md`
- Architecture L247-254 (P0 Cloudflare-DPDPA risk)

### Decision 2026-06-15-050: Story 1.15 substantive author-commit — Dokploy auto-deploy pipeline + multi-Pariwar provisioning `[SURFACE]`: the first global-scoped, permission-gated WRITE surface + the first CI→deploy leg.

**Decision type:** Story 1.15 — Dokploy auto-deploy + multi-Pariwar provisioning — `[SURFACE]` author-commit (bmad-dev-story phase).
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed; awaiting code review + trustee sign-off where applicable (the three reconciled runbooks need ≥2-trustee re-sign per their changelogs; ADR-0011 awaits trustee ratification).
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** pending

**Context:**
Story 1.15 lands the multi-Pariwar provisioning slice (FR-61/FR-62) + wires the live GitHub-Actions→Dokploy deploy leg (§5.3/§5.4). GCP + Dokploy are operator-confirmed available, so the deploy legs are LIVE-WIRED, but the dev agent has no live credentials (escrow-sealed) — hence the dev-agent-wires / operator-applies split.

**Decision:**
1. **`requireGlobalPermission(deps, key)`** — the global-scope permission pre-handler (sibling of `requirePermissionHook`), loads grants from the BYPASSRLS `servicePool`, checks at `global` scope. **Discharges D4-1.11a** and is retrofitted onto the audit-integrity surface (AC-1b; tightens CR-A-3 at the endpoint).
2. **Self-scoped provisioning write** — the handler mints the `pariwar_id` (UUID v4) and self-scopes to it (`openScopeTx` → `upsertPariwarPassport` → commit) so the RLS `WITH CHECK` passes; RLS = data boundary, the global gate = action boundary. No BYPASSRLS shortcut. No new migration (AC-8).
3. **Provisioning API + admin UI** — 3 global routes (`/api/v1/provisioning/...`) + the admin `/provisioning` page (exactly the 3 AC-4 controls; client-advisory `hasPariwarProvision` gate). New `@twt/contracts/pariwar-provisioning` contracts reusing the passport BrandingBundle/locale.
4. **Env-resolved `DeployTrigger` seam** (fake + live Dokploy-API client, `DEPLOY_TRIGGER_MODE`) + the path-scoped deploy-config reader (`/p/<id>/`).
5. **GitHub Actions → Dokploy API deploy model** (staging + prod): WIF-keyless build/push to Artifact Registry → POST Dokploy; prod ≥2-principal Environment gate + strictest WIF claim. Cloud SQL flat file extracted into `infra/gcp/modules/cloud-sql/` + reused per-env (D3-1.2 test holds); `infra/gcp/wif.tf` (D4-1.2).
6. **Dev-agent-wires / operator-applies split** — the dev agent authored + CI-validated everything; the operator runs the live `terraform apply` + Dokploy app creation + secret push.
7. **ADR-0011 drafted**; the three runbooks reconciled to AS-BUILT (material edit, ≥2-trustee re-sign); deferred-work reconciled (D3-1.2/D4-1.2/D3-1.12/W7/W8/W9/W12 wired; D4-1.5/D2-1.13 advanced; D1-1.13 STAYS GATED).

**Open follow-ups:**
- **TRACKED FOLLOW-ON (owner BigDev) — the live on-stage SM-1 2nd-Pariwar demo** (a real 2nd Pariwar serving real traffic on its own `/p/<id>/` URL) runs **post-merge once the operator confirms staging green**. AC-7 in-story proof = the dev/FAKE provision→deploy→status integration test; the on-stage demo is explicitly **NOT** closed by this story and **NOT** a merge gate. Recorded in `deferred-work.md`.
- The operator activation leg: `terraform apply` staging → smoke → prod; WIF binding; Dokploy app creation; per-env secret push; the D3-1.12 pg-boss CREATE grant (`infra/gcp/README.md` §Story 1.15, `docs/runbooks/deploy.md`).
- Cloudflare path-routing stays GATED on the §5.8a DPDPA legal review (D1-1.13, ADR-0010 OPEN) — do NOT assert DPDPA compliance.

### Decision 2026-06-15-049: Story 1.12 substantive author-commit — pg-boss job queue + idempotency keyed store `[PRIMITIVE]`: the canonical Postgres-backed background-work substrate (AR-5 / AR-58) every downstream queue consumer reuses. Bumps the monorepo Node baseline to 22.12.0+ and installs pg-boss 12.19.1 (DD-1 Option A); adds the mutable GLOBAL `idempotency_keys` table (migrations 0012/0013) with FORCE RLS + a writable `USING(true) WITH CHECK(true)` carve-out; the advisory-lock keyed store (`claim`/`recordResult`/`getResult`) in `@twt/domain`; a thin `packages/queue` (createQueueClient + JobEnvelope + queue-name registry); the `apps/jobs/src/boot.ts` worker runtime (health + SIGTERM drain + worker reg + cron); and the idempotency-TTL vacuum as the first pg-boss cron consumer. The mirror (D2-1.10) + integrity (D3-1.11a) cron graduations stay DEFERRED (DD-4).

**Decision type:** Story 1.12 — pg-boss job queue + idempotency keyed store — `[PRIMITIVE]` author-commit (bmad-dev-story phase).
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed; awaiting code review + trustee sign-off where applicable (the job-queue runbook needs ≥2-trustee sign-off per the runbooks ledger).
**Author:** BigDev (Solo Builder)
**Ratifying trustees:** pending

**Context:**
Story 1.12 installs the one queue + one idempotency contract so downstream epics (cycle spawn, reconciliation matcher, channel dispatcher, integrity-check, mirror push) reuse a single substrate instead of re-inventing background-work plumbing. Architecture commits pg-boss 12.x (§1.4), whose entire line requires Node ≥22.12.0 — but the repo baseline was pinned at Node 20, while the local dev machine was already on 22.22.2 (silent dev/CI drift). DD-1 (Option A) and DD-4 were confirmed by BigDev before implementation.

**Decision:**
1. **DD-1 Option A — Node baseline → 22.12.0+, pg-boss 12.19.1.** Bumped `.nvmrc`, root `engines.node`, every CI `setup-node` (`ci.yml` + `nightly-integrity.yml`), and all four Dockerfiles `node:20-alpine → node:22-alpine`. This realigns CI with the local machine and adopts the architecture-committed version (Node 20 nears EOL). `code-escrow-mirror.yml` has no `node-version` pin (skipped, as scoped).
2. **DD-2 — `idempotency_keys` is a MUTABLE GLOBAL table.** No `pariwar_id` column (callers namespace keys); ENABLE+FORCE RLS for Story-1.6 regime-consistency with a permissive `ALL USING(true) WITH CHECK(true)` carve-out (twt_app writes in the request path). NO reject-mutation triggers (it is mutated by recordResult/reclaim/vacuum) — the migration 0004 `role_grants` mutable-grant pattern, not the 0008/0009 read-only ledger pattern. The rejected v1 alternative (a `pariwar_id` column + scoped RLS) is recorded as a deferred follow-up (D1-1.12).
3. **Refinement over the story text — twt_service also gets the table GRANT.** The story said "GRANT … TO twt_app; service role inherits via BYPASSRLS." BYPASSRLS waives only RLS-policy evaluation, NOT table-privilege checks, so the worker-path vacuum DELETE needs an explicit `SELECT,INSERT,UPDATE,DELETE` GRANT to twt_service (the 0009 precedent). Migration 0013 grants both roles.
4. **DD-3 — keyed store in `@twt/domain` (`idempotency.*`); the reusable queue contract in a new `packages/queue`.** `createQueueClient` is the only sanctioned pg-boss constructor (schema isolation + SSL posture + mandatory error handler baked in once); `JobEnvelope` is the ALS-rehydration contract; `QUEUE_NAMES` is the registry. `packages/queue` has no `@twt/domain` dependency. Enqueue-from-apps/api is OUT of scope (no real consumer until Epic 7).
5. **DD-4 — mirror (D2-1.10) + integrity (D3-1.11a) cron graduations stay DEFERRED.** The cron substrate is proven by the self-contained idempotency-TTL vacuum (AC-5); the two crons still need a durable watermark table + prod creds, re-affirmed in `deferred-work.md`.
6. **DD-5 documented — pg-boss `singletonKey` (enqueue-dedupe) is NOT the keyed store** (claim/recordResult/getResult; both callers observe the same result). They compose; the runbook records the distinction.

**Open follow-ups:**
- Code review by a different LLM (recommended); merge `story/1.12-pg-boss-job-queue-idempotency` → main + watch CI on Node 22.
- ≥2-trustee sign-off of `docs/runbooks/job-queue-operations.md` per the runbooks ledger.
- D1-1.12 (per-tenant key isolation via `pariwar_id` + composite PK) if a future consumer needs strict isolation; D2-1.10 / D3-1.11a cron graduations; the prod first-start CREATE grant for the pg-boss connection role.

**References:**
- `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/1-12-pg-boss-job-queue-idempotency-keyed-store.md` — story file
- `packages/queue/` · `packages/domain/src/idempotency/` · `packages/domain/migrations/0012_idempotency-keys.sql` + `0013_idempotency-keys-rls.sql` · `apps/jobs/src/boot.ts`
- `docs/runbooks/job-queue-operations.md` — operations runbook
- architecture.md §1.4 / §5.9 / §5.11 / §context-propagation · epics.md L1214-1230 (AR-5 / AR-58)

### Decision 2026-06-14-048: Story 1.11b substantive author-commit — Trustee-facing audit-log integrity-verification UI `[SURFACE]`: the first real admin SPA in the repo (Vite 7 + React 19 + Tailwind v4 + Radix + TanStack Router/Query + RHF/Zod), bootstrapped minimally to host the trustee "Verify audit-log integrity" page that CONSUMES Story 1.11a's `POST /verify-integrity`. Adds three backend endpoints (`GET /auth/session` grant introspection, `GET /audit/integrity-checks` history, `POST .../:checkId/acknowledge`) + migration 0011 (separate append-only `audit_integrity_acknowledgements` table). DD-1…DD-7 resolved per their recommended paths (DD-2/DD-5/DD-6 were the confirmed scope forks). Two recorded deviations (code-based routing; session grants in Query not Zustand) + one in-passing 1.11a defect fix.

**Decision type:** Story 1.11b — trustee audit-integrity UI + first admin-SPA bootstrap — `[SURFACE]` author-commit (bmad-dev-story phase).
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed. Cold-mirror pointer, helpdesk/ticketing, full client codegen, `packages/ui`+`packages/tokens` extraction, passkey-enrollment/reset UI, and the endpoint-side `audit.verify` RBAC gate are deferred (triggers in deferred-work.md, Story 1.11b section).
**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev)
**Ratifying trustees:** _pending_

**Context:**
Story 1.11a shipped the on-demand verification endpoint + the verdict ledger but no read path and no UI. AC source: epics.md L1195-1212. Because `apps/admin/` was a `tsc` placeholder and `packages/{api-client,ui,tokens}` are stubs, 1.11b ALSO bootstraps the admin SPA shell. Seven DDs flagged how much net-new foundation to lay; BigDev confirmed DD-2/DD-5/DD-6 on their recommended paths (2026-06-14).

**Decision:**
1. **DD-1 — bootstrap the architecture-committed admin stack, MINIMALLY.** Vite 7 + React 19 + TS + Tailwind v4 (CSS-first, `@tailwindcss/vite`, NO `tailwind.config.ts`) + Radix + TanStack Query v5 (cache-disabled reads, §4.5) + TanStack Router + RHF/Zod, plus a thin app shell + this one module under `apps/admin/src/modules/audit-integrity/`. Deferred: `packages/ui`/`packages/tokens` extraction (Tailwind/Radix used directly), full client codegen, IndexedDB persister (N/A — strong-consistency). `apps/admin` `build` switched `tsc` → `tsc --noEmit && vite build`; Vitest+jsdom+RTL added; Dockerfile now builds the bundle + serves it via nginx with SPA fallback.
2. **DD-2 (scope fork, CONFIRMED) — minimal real `/login`.** `src/routes/LoginPage.tsx` drives the existing 1.9 API: password first factor (RHF + `LoginRequest`) → `mfa_required` → passkey-authenticate (`@simplewebauthn/browser`) OR recovery-consume. Enrollment/reset/step-up UI deferred (already-enrolled admin). CSRF posture matches ADR-0009 (Origin+SameSite baseline; double-submit only on logout).
3. **DD-3 — GLOBAL `GET /api/v1/audit/integrity-checks?limit&triggerSource` in `modules/audit-log`.** `requireAdminSession`-gated, reads via `deps.servicePool` (NOT `requirePermissionHook` — a global route has no `scopeTx` → 500, the documented 1.11a landmine). Returns most-recent-first list items = the `AuditIntegrityCheckResult` verdict PLUS its most-recent acknowledgement (or null), so banner persistence derives in one read. New contracts `AuditIntegrityCheckListItem` / `AuditIntegrityCheckList` (additive; the 1.11a verdict contract + type-assignability test untouched). "Last automated check" derives client-side (trigger ≠ on_demand).
4. **DD-4 — red-banner fields derive from verdict + history; cold-mirror is an explicit deferral.** Failing row = `firstBrokenAuditId`/`Seq`; prior-valid row = `endAuditId`/`Seq`; tamper-suspect window = [last `chainValid=true` check's `verifiedAt`, this check's `verifiedAt`]; cold-mirror pointer = the last provably-good HOT-chain state, clearly labelled, with a "cold-mirror cross-verification: deferred (D1-1.11a)" line — NO fabricated pointer. Pure derivation in `derive.ts` (unit-tested).
5. **DD-5 (scope fork, CONFIRMED) — separate append-only acknowledgement table + endpoint.** Migration 0011 adds `audit_integrity_acknowledgements` (`check_id` FK, `acknowledged_at/by`, `ticket_ref NOT NULL`) with append-only triggers + `USING(true)` RLS + grants, mirroring 0008/0009 — so `audit_integrity_checks` stays STRICTLY immutable. `POST .../:checkId/acknowledge { ticketRef }` (`requireAdminSession` only — NO `app.csrfProtection`; ADR-0009). Banner persistence is client-derived (latest `chainValid=false` AND no acknowledgement → red); acknowledge → invalidate → clears. The free-text `ticketRef` IS the v1 "investigation ticket opened" artifact (helpdesk FR-52 deferred).
6. **DD-6 (scope fork, CONFIRMED) — minimal global `GET /api/v1/auth/session` → `{ userId, nationalGrants[] }`.** Handler reads `role_grants WHERE user_id=$1` via `deps.servicePool` (BYPASSRLS), maps each role → permission keys via `defaultRoleBundles` (`@twt/domain/rbac`), unions the keys of rows at `scope_dimension='global'` (⚠ "national" = `global` per ADR-0008; NOT a `'national'` value). The SPA gates the nav entry + `/audit/integrity` route on `nationalGrants.includes('audit.verify')`. Advisory only — `requireAdminSession` is the real boundary; endpoint-side RBAC stays D4-1.11a.
7. **DD-7 — thin hand-written typed `fetch` client reusing `@twt/contracts` Zod.** `src/api/client.ts` parses every response with the contract schemas (single type source); TanStack Query hooks wrap verify/list/session/acknowledge. Full OpenAPI→client codegen + tool ADR deferred (D3-1.11b).
8. **Recorded DEVIATIONS.** (a) **Code-based routing** instead of file-based `routeTree.gen.ts` codegen (D8-1.11b) — keeps the `tsc → vite build` gate + CI deterministic for a 3-route surface; the committed TanStack Router stack (§4.7) is unchanged. (b) **Session grants in TanStack Query**, not a separate Zustand store (D7-1.11b) — the session is a server read; §4.3 places server state in Query.
9. **In-passing 1.11a defect fix (Closed by [edit]).** `verifyChainWalk`'s `brokenAt` reported `startSeq=<head>` but `endSeq=null` on a head-truncation, which migration 0010's `boundary_pair_coherent` CHECK rejects → `verifyAuditChain` THREW instead of persisting the failed verdict (defeating head-truncation detection + breaking AC-4's head-truncation banner). Fixed to report a coherent `start=null/end=null` when nothing verified; SHAPE unchanged. Also fixed a flaky `@twt/jobs` live-DB test-file-parallelism interleaving (`fileParallelism:false`) and a pre-existing `scope-tx.spec` `$2`-reuse bug, to add `@twt/api` to the CI integration filter so the new endpoint tests ride CI (Task 10.3).

**Open follow-ups:**
- Deferred (Resolved via explicit deferral), Story 1.11b section of deferred-work.md: D1 cold-mirror pointer, D2 helpdesk/ticketing, D3 client codegen, D4 ui/tokens extraction, D5 enrollment/reset UI, D6 endpoint-side RBAC (D4-1.11a), D7 Zustand store, D8 file-based routing.
- Trustee ratification of this author-commit.

**References:**
- `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/1-11b-trustee-facing-audit-log-integrity-verification-ui.md` — story file

- Architecture §4.1/§4.2/§4.3/§4.5/§4.7/§4.9/§4.10; Decision 2026-06-14-047 (Story 1.11a — the endpoint this UI consumes)

### Decision 2026-06-14-047: Story 1.11a substantive author-commit — Audit-log integrity-verification primitive: the job that WALKS the global hash chain Story 1.10 built. `audit_integrity_checks` verdict ledger (GLOBAL, append-only, USING(true) RLS) + the chunked walk with genesis-anchor + cross-chunk stitch (closes the CR-D2-1.10 landmine) + three triggers reduced to one `verifyAuditChain` fn (cron / on-demand endpoint / post-mirror) + provider-fluid observability sink + alerter seams. Verifies the HOT Postgres chain via the service pool (DD-1); cold-mirror-from-separate-project verification + live observability/alerting + prod nightly run deferred as recorded graduations.

**Decision type:** Story 1.11a — audit-log integrity-verification substrate — `[PRIMITIVE]` author-commit (bmad-dev-story phase).
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed. Verify-from-cold-mirror (separate GCP project read SA), live Cloud-Monitoring observability + alert-policy wiring, the prod-pointed nightly run, and the full RBAC `audit.verify` gate are deferred (triggers recorded in deferred-work.md).
**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev)
**Ratifying trustees:** _pending_

**Context:**
Story 1.10 produced a *verifiable* global hash chain (`audit_log_entries`) + a *pure* `verifyChainSegment`, and deliberately handed five `CR-D*-1.10` forks forward. Story 1.11a builds the JOB that walks the chain end-to-end on a daily schedule + on-demand + after every mirror push, records the verdict, and publishes/alerts — so tampering is caught by automated detection, not manual discovery. The five DDs were resolved per their recommended paths.

**Decision:**
1. **DD-1 — verification SOURCE = HOT Postgres chain (now).** `verifyAuditChain` reads `audit_log_entries` in ascending `seq` via the BYPASSRLS service pool (the true GLOBAL chain), identical read posture to the 1.10 mirror. The walk is written SOURCE-AGNOSTIC (consumes a `ChunkReader`), so the architecture's ideal — verify the COLD GCS mirror from a separate-project read SA in its own execution environment (§1.5 L850-853 / §2.10) — is a NEW reader, not a rewrite. **Deferred graduation** (the mirror is a CI fake today; live GCS apply deferred per D1-1.10); a future mirror-JSONL reader must reconstitute `recordedAt` with `new Date(...)` (CR-D10-1.10).
2. **DD-2 — chunked walk + genesis anchor + cross-chunk stitch (the central correctness call).** `verifyChainSegment` by design does NOT check `row[0]`'s predecessor linkage (hash-chain.ts:148-152), so naive per-chunk verification silently misses a break at a chunk boundary / a head-truncation (CR-D2-1.10). The job-level walk closes both WITHOUT touching the pure function: (a) **genesis anchor** — the lowest-seq row of the whole chain must have `prevAuditHash === null`, else the head was truncated → broken; (b) **cross-chunk stitch** — carry the prior chunk's last `auditHash` and assert `chunk[0].prevAuditHash === carriedPrevHash` before `verifyChainSegment`. The optional `predecessorLinkageVerified` signature change was NOT taken (lower blast radius to keep the stitch in the job). Proven by deterministic pure unit tests (mid-chunk tamper, boundary deletion, head-truncation) + live-DB synthetic-tamper tests.
3. **DD-3 — `audit_integrity_checks` GLOBAL table, append-only, USING(true) RLS, 0008/0009 split.** The chain is GLOBAL (one `seq`, no `pariwar_id`), so the verdict ledger is a GLOBAL table (identity/auth carve-out class, R2): ENABLE+FORCE RLS + a `USING(true)` SELECT carve-out (the visible "nothing to tenant-scope" line, keeping the Story-1.6 every-twt_app-table-is-FORCE-RLS invariant whole); `GRANT SELECT`→twt_app (the 1.11b reader), `GRANT INSERT,SELECT`→twt_service (the writer); append-only `*_reject_mutation` triggers (a verdict cannot be un-recorded). Migrations 0008 (table+triggers) / 0009 (RLS+grants+self-test) mirror the 0006/0007 split, both carrying the `⚠ DO NOT REGENERATE` header. **Refinement of DD-3 (recorded):** `start_seq` / `end_seq` / `start_audit_id` / `end_audit_id` are NULLABLE (DD-3 said notNull) so a run against an EMPTY chain records `chain_valid=true, rows_verified=0` with NULL boundaries; every non-empty run populates them. The AC-named `*_audit_id` columns are resolved from the boundary rows alongside the chain-native `*_seq` the verifier returns.
4. **DD-4 — three triggers reduce to one function; pg-boss cron deferred.** `verifyAuditChain` is the single function; the triggers are (a) daily `.github/workflows/nightly-integrity.yml` GH-Actions cron — `30 20 * * *` UTC = 02:00 IST (documented) — running the synthetic-tamper gate + an end-to-end walk; (b) on-demand `POST /api/v1/audit/verify-integrity` (GLOBAL, gated `requireAdminSession`); (c) a direct call at the end of the mirror CLI (`triggerSource=post_mirror`), wired in the entrypoint so the pure `pushNewAuditLinesToMirror` stays uncoupled. The canonical pg-boss/Cloud-Scheduler cron + the prod-pointed run are the **Story 1.12 graduation** (D2-1.10; pg-boss not installed).
5. **DD-5 — provider-fluid observability sink + alerter seams; live wiring deferred.** `IntegrityObservabilitySink.publish` (AC-4) + `IntegrityAlerter.alertChainBroken` (AC-5) ports + structured-log fakes (default) + in-memory capturing fakes (tests) + `resolveIntegrity{Sink,Alerter}FromEnv` (`INTEGRITY_OBSERVABILITY_MODE` fake|live, mirroring `MIRROR_MODE`). `live` fails closed — the Cloud Monitoring adapter is the **Category-5 graduation** (§5.6).
6. **Global-RBAC gate posture.** The on-demand endpoint gates on `requireAdminSession` ONLY, NOT `requirePermissionHook`: the RBAC hook requires `request.scopeTx` (set from `/:pariwarId/`), which a GLOBAL route has no param for → it hard-throws 500. The `audit.verify` permission key exists (Story 1.8); upgrading the gate to the full RBAC check is **deferred** until a global-scope preHandler exists. The `/p/:pariwarId/audit/verify-probe` placeholder stays in place (it exercises the scoped RBAC second-guard independently).

**Open follow-ups:**
- CR-D2-1.10 / CR-D10-1.10 / CR-D11-1.10 — **Closed by [edit]** in this story (stitch+anchor; source-agnostic walk + Date-reconstitution note; gap-tolerance proven). CR-D3-1.10 / CR-D6-1.10 (cross-language ms-precision + 64-hex format) remain deferred to ADR-0004 / the 1.11b SDK gen (v1 is JS-only).
- Deferred (Resolved via explicit deferral): verify-from-cold-mirror (DD-1), live observability/alerting (DD-5 → Category 5), prod-pointed nightly run + pg-boss cron (DD-4 → Story 1.12), optional `predecessorLinkageVerified` field (DD-2), RBAC `audit.verify` gate upgrade (global-scope preHandler).
- Trustee ratification of this author-commit.

**References:**
- `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/1-11a-audit-log-integrity-verification-primitive.md` — story file
- `apps/jobs/src/audit/integrity-check.ts` + `integrity-observability.ts` + `integrity-cli.ts`; `apps/api/src/modules/audit-log/index.ts`; `packages/domain/src/schema/audit_integrity_checks.ts` + `policies/audit-integrity-checks-rls.ts`; migrations `0008` / `0009`; `packages/contracts/src/audit/integrity-check.ts`; `.github/workflows/nightly-integrity.yml`
- Architecture §1.5 L839-902 (integrity-check job), §2.10/§2.10a (separate-project read creds), §5.6 (observability — Category 5)
- Decision 2026-06-12-046 (Story 1.10 — the chain this story walks); deferred-work.md CR-D2/D3/D6/D10/D11-1.10

### Decision 2026-06-12-046: Story 1.10 substantive author-commit — Tamper-evident audit log: `audit_log_entries` hash chain + append-only triggers + RLS + BYPASSRLS service-role writer + 6h off-site WORM mirror (`[PRIMITIVE]`) — canonical-JSON moved to `@twt/domain` (DD-1); single global chain serialized by advisory lock (DD-2); real `twt_service` BYPASSRLS posture + service pool (DD-3); ships non-partitioned, W16 verified (DD-4); mirror push fn + fake/live split, 6h cron deferred to pg-boss 1.12 (DD-5). Wires the auth/KMS/cross-tenant seams to the real sink; integrity-check JOB + trustee UI are 1.11a/1.11b.

**Decision type:** Story 1.10 — tamper-evident audit-log substrate — `[PRIMITIVE]` author-commit (bmad-dev-story phase).
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed; ADR-0004 amended (canonical-JSON home move). Live `terraform apply` + the retention LOCK + pg-boss 6h cron deferred.
**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev)
**Ratifying trustees:** _pending_

**Context:**
FR-47 + AR-9/10 + the architectural-freeze immutability property (freeze-table row 5) require every privileged action to emit a tamper-evident, hash-chained audit line, hot-persisted to Postgres and 6-hourly mirrored to an Object-Retention-Locked GCS bucket in a separate IAM tenancy. Five design forks the prior stories deferred (DD-1…DD-5) were resolved per their recommendations.

**Decision:**
1. **DD-1 — canonical-JSON home.** Moved `canonical-json.ts` → `@twt/domain` (single home; pure fn, zero deps); `@twt/events` re-exports it via a thin shim (no consumer change). Avoids the `@twt/domain`↔`@twt/events` cycle the domain-level producers (KMS hook, cross-tenant) would create. ADR-0004 amended.
2. **DD-2 — one global chain.** `audit_log_entries` with `seq bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY` ordering; `audit_hash = SHA-256(prev_hash_feed || canonicalJsonStringify(auditRowDigestInput(row)))` computed in TS (AC-6; no plpgsql). Writer serializes read-tail→insert with `pg_advisory_xact_lock` (closes W8-CR1.6 hot-row). Genesis: `prev_audit_hash = NULL` in-column, `GENESIS_PREV_HASH` (64 zeros) as the hash feed. `seq` is EXCLUDED from the digest (DB-assigned-after-hash per DD-2; unpredictable pre-insert since seq is consumed on rollback) — chain order is enforced by prev→this linkage, seq is the walk index. The single `auditRowDigestInput` projection is used by BOTH writer and `verifyChainSegment`.
3. **DD-3 — service role.** Real `twt_service` BYPASSRLS prod-login posture documented in migration 0007 (the GROUP stays NOBYPASSRLS; the login MEMBER carries BYPASSRLS); `AppDeps.servicePool` + `runAsCrossTenant` `servicePool` param exposed; dev/CI reuses the superuser pool. Closes W2-CR1.6 (the explanatory comment) + D9-1.6 (shape).
4. **DD-4 — partitioning.** Ships NON-partitioned (events_log precedent). W16 verified: pg_partman IS on the Cloud SQL supported-extensions list; native declarative partitioning needs no pg_partman anyway. The chain is over `seq` (partition-agnostic) → deferral is safe.
5. **DD-5 — mirror.** Pure `pushNewAuditLinesToMirror` + CLI in `apps/jobs/src/audit/`; `MirrorTarget`/`WatermarkStore` ports + in-memory fakes + GCS adapter (`MIRROR_MODE` fake|live, mirroring `KMS_TEST_MODE`); append-only seq-encoded segment objects (no overwrites). Terraform commits the separate-project WORM bucket + write-only SA + authoritative objectCreator binding (AC-4). 6h cron → pg-boss (Story 1.12); live apply deferred.
6. **Append-only + RLS (AC-2/AC-8).** Migration 0006 installs reject-mutation triggers (UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE); migration 0007 a SELECT-only tenant-isolation policy (tenants never write audit lines).
7. **Seam wiring (AC-7).** `createAuditLogSink` (AuthAuditSink, auto-covers rbac denial + scope.change) + `createKmsAuditHook` + `runAsCrossTenant` re-key (events_log → audit_log_entries) with the W1-CR1.6 two-transaction split (audit committed first, fails-closed). No double-write to events_log (Task 6.3 / D3-1.3 resolved). NO new `audit.*` registry entries.
8. **Contract (AC-1).** `AuditLogEntryContract` `.strict()`, standalone (no EventLogContract base; D13-1.4 closed), registered as the OpenAPI `AuditLogEntry` component (no paths — reads are 1.11b).

**Open follow-ups:**
- **Deferred (triggers in deferred-work.md Story 1.10 section):** D1-1.10 live terraform apply + retention LOCK; D2-1.10 6h cron + durable watermark → pg-boss 1.12; D3-1.10 prod `SERVICE_DATABASE_URL`; D4-1.10 partitioning → scale story; D5-1.10 `canonical-context.ts` delegation → encryption refactor.
- **Pre-existing (NOT 1.10 regressions, verified on main):** 2 `contracts/auth.test.ts` `.min(12)` mismatches + 2 `apps/api/admin-auth.spec.ts` lockout/logout — Story-1.9 territory.
- Per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: engineering + all D2-1.9/D2-1.8/D10-1.5/D5-1.6/D2-1.6/D9-1.6/D13-1.4/D13-1.2/D7-1.3/D13-1.5/D3-1.3/W16/W1/W2/W6/W8-CR1.6 = Closed by [edit]; the live-apply / cron / prod-credential / partitioning remainders = Resolved via explicit deferral.

**References:**
- `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/1-10-tamper-evident-audit-log-hash-chain-6h-off-site-mirror.md` — story file
- `docs/adr/ADR-0004-canonical-json.md` — amended (canonical-JSON home move)
- `infra/gcp/audit-mirror.tf` + `docs/runbooks/audit-mirror-attestation.md`

### Decision 2026-06-12-045: Story 1.9 substantive author-commit — Admin authentication + the `apps/api` Fastify framework landing (email/password Argon2id+pepper + WebAuthn v13 passkey + step-up OTP; `users` global identity + auth tables migration 0005; scope-resolution + RBAC HTTP adapters; ADR-0009 drafted) — closes the apps/api-landing deferral cluster (D3-1.8/D4-1.6/D4-1.7/D4-1.8/D3-1.4/D14-1.5(b)); flags one correct-course patch (R1) + surfaces the identity-table RLS posture (R2) for architecture confirmation; defers SMS delivery → 5.6/5.9, audit sink → 1.10, Turnstile → 1.13, admin UI → post-1.17, api-client fast-follow.

**Decision type:** Story 1.9 — admin authentication (email/password + WebAuthn passkey + step-up OTP) — `[SURFACE]` substrate author-commit (bmad-dev-story phase).
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed; ADR-0009 `drafted` (awaiting trustee + architecture confirmation of the R2 identity-table RLS posture).
**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev)
**Ratifying trustees:** _pending_

**Context:**
Story 1.9 stands up `apps/api` (Fastify) — the first runnable HTTP surface — and proves it by shipping admin auth end-to-end. It creates the `users` global identity table the system has waited on, mounts Story 1.8's framework-agnostic `requirePermission` + the §2.5 scope-resolution middleware as real Fastify pre-handlers, and discharges the apps/api-landing deferral cluster. Four reconciliations were surfaced (not silently resolved); R1 (session model) + R2 (identity-table RLS posture) are load-bearing.

**Decision:**
1. **Fastify substrate (AC-5)** — `buildServer(deps)` factory + the committed §3 plugin/middleware/module tree; request-context ALS + `encryptionContextStorage` hydration; error-mapping → `ErrorResponse` envelope.
2. **R1 — session model `@fastify/session` + Postgres store** (idle 12h / absolute 7d, SameSite=Lax, session-id rotation, revoke-by-row-delete) is canonical for admin auth — supersedes the epic AC's JWT/refresh wording (that is mobile/member, §2.4 → Story 3.2); "2 trusted devices" → ≤2 WebAuthn passkeys. **Correct-course note** flags epics.md L1147.
3. **Argon2id + pepper (AC-1)** — `@node-rs/argon2`, peppered via the `secret` keyed mode, pepper from Secret Manager; OWASP-2026 params (m=64MiB/t=3/p=1) with an annual review cadence. Email = Tier-1 ciphertext + Tier-2 blind index (never a plaintext column).
4. **WebAuthn v13 (AC-2)** — SimpleWebAuthn `WebAuthnCredential`; injectable provider seam; counter-regression rejection; enrollment ceremony (full session OR single-use signed link; password-only never enrolls); 10 hashed one-time recovery codes at first enrollment. Single-use links via stateless state-binding (no 0006 migration).
5. **R2 — identity/auth tables are GLOBAL carve-out family** (ENABLE+FORCE RLS + `USING(true)` for `twt_app`), NOT pariwar-scoped — login precedes scope, so the scoped construct would return 0 rows. Retro FKs `role_grants.user_id → users.id` (D4-1.8) + `pariwar_passport.created_by → users.id` (D4-1.7) added in migration 0005. **Surfaced for architecture confirmation.**
6. **Scope-resolution + RBAC adapter (AC-6)** — strict-UUID re-parse, scope tx (runs as `twt_app`), membership 404, `requirePermission` pre-handler → 403; **W9-CR1.6 implemented** (assert-scope-set + in-context guard). Closes D4-1.6.
7. **R3 — step-up OTP** mechanism (hash-only, TTL 3min, single-use, invalidate-on-next, rate-limited) + gating middleware; delivery behind `StepUpOtpDeliveryPort` dev stub — real SMS-DLT is Story 5.6/5.9.
8. **R4 — audit seam** (default structured log; FR-47 hash-chain sink is Story 1.10) + Turnstile no-op seam (Story 1.13).
9. **Contracts + OpenAPI (AC-8)** — `.strict()` auth contracts + `UserIdSchema`; the first real OpenAPI `paths` (determinism byte-stable); D14-1.4 = build-time-script; api-client (D2-1.4) deferred to a fast-follow.
10. **Dep-version reconciliation** — `fastify-type-provider-zod` pinned ^4 (v5/v6 need Zod 4; repo is classic Zod 3); `fastify-zod-openapi` dropped (competing type provider). Recorded in ADR-0009.

**Open follow-ups:**
- **Correct-course (one source patch):** epics.md L1147 — admin "90d refresh / 15min access" reconciled away to the §2.4 admin-web session model. Surfaced, NOT silently edited.
- **Architecture confirmation (R2):** the identity/auth RLS posture (carve-out USING(true) vs dedicated auth role vs no-RLS-with-grants) is a real decision; Story 1.9 chose carve-out for Passport-precedent consistency + defense-in-depth.
- **ADR-0009 ratification** — `drafted` → `under-trustee-review` → `ratified`. Closure-language posture: engineering substrate Closed by [edit]; ADR/architecture ratification Resolved via explicit deferral.
- New deferred-work D-items (SMS step-up delivery → 5.6/5.9; FR-47 audit sink → 1.10; Turnstile → 1.13; admin web UI → post-1.17; api-client fast-follow).

**References:**

- `docs/adr/ADR-0009-admin-authentication.md` — drafted ADR

### Decision 2026-06-11-044: Story 1.8 substantive author-commit — RBAC permission model v1 (permission-key catalog + reconciled scope enum + `(role, scope)` grant tuple + containment + 12 seeded roles + fail-closed guard at `packages/domain/src/rbac/`; `role_grants` scoped table + RLS migration 0004; `packages/contracts/src/rbac/` transport contracts; ADR-0008 drafted) — closes D10-1.4(rbac leg); flags two correct-course patches; defers geo-tree seam + audit sink + HTTP adapter + role_grants FK.

**Decision type:** Story 1.8 — RBAC permission-keys + scope-dimensions + 12 seeded roles — substrate author-commit (bmad-dev-story phase).
**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed; ADR-0008 `drafted` (awaiting trustee ratification; OQ-3 gates the role-matrix before production seed).
**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev)
**Ratifying trustees:** _pending_

**Context:**
Story 1.8 lands the authorization substrate — the SECOND guard on every privileged action (RLS is the first, Story 1.6). A pure-domain primitive (permission-key catalog + scope model + 12 declarative role bundles + fail-closed `requirePermission`/`hasPermission`), the first `packages/contracts/src/rbac/` transport contracts, and the `role_grants` storage table. Three source-doc inconsistencies had to be reconciled (not silently resolved, per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]); the scope-enum one is load-bearing (neither source enum can express all 12 seeded roles). It deliberately stops short of the HTTP layer (apps/api has no framework — Story 1.9) and the FR-47 audit sink (Story 1.10).

**Decision:**
1. **Key format `<resource>.<action>`** (architecture §2.6 L1479 + all grounded keys) — reconciles the epic's backwards `verb.resource` (epics.md L1127). Branded `PermissionKey` + smart constructor + versioned, append-only `PERMISSION_CATALOG` seeded with the 9 grounded keys only.
2. **Package location `packages/domain/src/rbac/`** — reconciles the epic's non-existent `packages/rbac` shorthand against architecture §2.6 L1481/L1483 + §3.13 L2420 + every prior primitive's placement.
3. **Scope enum `global | pariwar | state | district | block | self`** — the UNION the 12 roles require (`national`→`global`; keep `block`; add `self`), reconciling two individually-incomplete sources (architecture/FR-45 omits `self`; epic omits `block`). Committed as one canonical `SCOPE_DIMENSIONS` tuple driving both the domain union and the `scope_dimension` pgEnum.
4. **Scope = `(dimension, value)` + hierarchical containment** (not a flat enum compare) per FR-45's Anita/Patna-vs-Vaishali example (prd.md L754); the geo-tree resolver is an injectable seam with a fail-closed deny-deeper default (canonical org tree lands Epic 3).
5. **Grant tuple `(user_id, pariwar_id, role)`** + cross-Pariwar composition (effective grants filtered by active `pariwarId`; cross-scope inheritance forbidden; `global` grants are the sole cross-Pariwar exception).
6. **12 declarative role bundles** — provisional pending OQ-3, editable (FR-44), idempotent `seedRoles()`; referential integrity (every bundle key ∈ catalog) unit-tested; sparse/empty rows are correct.
7. **`role` column `text`** (not pgEnum) so OQ-3 can revise the set without a migration; `scope_dimension` IS a pgEnum (canonical/ratified).
8. **Fail-closed guard** — default-deny on every uncertain path; structured 403 `{ actorId, permissionKey, requiredScope, targetLocator }` mapped to the `ErrorResponse` envelope (by domain-local shape, no `@twt/contracts` import); injectable `onAuthorizationDenied` audit SEAM (FR-47 sink is Story 1.10); framework-agnostic (HTTP adapter is Story 1.9).
9. **`role_grants` table LANDED now** (vs defer) — scoped (NOT carve-out) RLS via the Story 1.6 closed-failure construct; migration 0004 ENABLE+FORCE RLS + GRANT incl. DELETE (grants are revocable); added to `cross-pariwar-leak.spec.ts` as a must-return-0 table.

**Open follow-ups:**
- **Correct-course (two one-line source patches):** architecture §2.6 L1484 + epics.md L1127 — adopt the canonical scope enum `global | pariwar | state | district | block | self`; epics.md L1127 — adopt `<resource>.<action>` key order. Surfaced for ratification; NOT silently edited.
- **OQ-3** — Trustee Panel confirms/revises the 12-role set + permission matrix pre-launch ("Blocks: RBAC seed in production"). The bundles are provisional until then.
- **ADR-0008 ratification** — `drafted` → `under-trustee-review` post-merge → `ratified` (engineering tooling + security-relevant; light-touch ratification acceptable). Closure-language posture: engineering substrate Closed by [edit]; ADR ratification Resolved via explicit deferral.
- New deferred-work D-items (geo-tree containment seam → Epic 3; FR-47 audit sink → 1.10; HTTP-middleware + scope-resolution adapter → 1.9; `role_grants` FK to users → 1.9+).

**References:**
- `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/1-8-rbac-permission-keys-scope-dimensions-12-seeded-roles.md` — story file
- `docs/adr/ADR-0008-rbac-permission-model.md` — drafted ADR
- `docs/knowledge-transfer/adr-index.md` — Section A row for ADR-0008 (new `drafted` row; total 126 → 127)
- `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md` — D10-1.4 (rbac leg, Closed by edit) + new Story 1.8 D-items
- architecture §2.6 L1476-1496 + §3.13 L2406-2421; epics.md Story 1.8 L1117-1133; prd.md FR-44/45/46 + OQ-3
- Decision 2026-06-11-043 (prior — Story 1.7); [[feedback_architecture_vs_prd_boundary]] + [[feedback_architecture_vs_adr_boundary]] + [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]
