### Decision 2026-06-05-035: Phase-0 provisional closure ratified per QX.2 — substitute paths accepted across Phase-0 portfolio; Phase-0 prereq-gate fires; Epic 1 substrate work unblocked; full closure deferred per `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md` cross-Story coupling

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — Phase-0 closure meta-event.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05 by ≥2-trustee panel (Dhiraj Rahul + Kalpana Bharti) via `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/phase-0-trustee-questionnaire.md` sign-off (74 trustee decisions across original + 1st-addendum + 2nd-addendum closed).

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of QX.1 + QX.2 + QX.3 + Phase-0 closure context.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1), Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2). Both signed all three sign-off blocks of `phase-0-trustee-questionnaire.md` dated 2026-06-04 / 2026-06-05.

**Context.** The Phase-0 trustee questionnaire (32 original + 28 first-addendum + 14 second-addendum = 74 decisions across 13 stories) was authored 2026-06-04 in response to the Story 0.12 Task 9 trustee-acknowledgement requirement (Decision 2026-06-04-016 supersedes to ratified at Decision 2026-06-05-028 below). The questionnaire surfaced trustee-decision items across all Phase-0 stories: in-progress (0.1, 0.2, 0.3), review (0.12), and `done`-per-framework but with `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION_` Tasks 7+ (0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 0.10, 0.11, 0.13, 0.14, 0.15). Per QX.2 = "Provisionally closed", Phase-0 stories accept substitute paths where Story 0.6 backup-engineer execution / Story 1.10 audit-mirror substrate / Story 1.5 KEK-tier substrate are pre-conditions for full closure. Per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`, each Story's ledger row records "Provisionally closed by [edit/sign-off]; full closure deferred per [...]" rather than collapsing the two states.

**Decision body:**

1. **QX.1 — Story 0.6 backup engineer urgency.** Trustees prioritize the Story 0.6 backup engineer's first table-top execution for the next phase post Phase-0 sign-off. Story 0.6 framework-leg fully closes via Decision 2026-06-05-022 below; named-engineer outreach begins per Q6.3 hybrid path; AC-2 activation-scenario test (Story 0.1 AC-4 path 1 discharge) follows once Story 0.6 Task 10 lands the contracted engineer.

2. **QX.2 — Phase-0 closure language.** Phase-0 closes as **Provisionally closed** per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`. Substitute paths accepted; full closure deferred per `deferred-work.md`. Phase-0 prereq-gate per `epics.md` line 564 ("Phase-0 prereq gates gate all engineering") fires; Epic 1 substrate work (Story 1.1 Turborepo bootstrap onwards) is unblocked subject to per-Story provisional-closure dispositions enumerated in Decisions 2026-06-05-017 through 2026-06-05-034.

3. **QX.3 — Trustee ratification mechanics.** Inline trustee initials in `phase-0-trustee-questionnaire.md` + document committed to repo + this `.decision-log.md` transcription event (Decisions 017-035) constitute the canonical ratification record. Per-Story Decision supersessions reference back to the questionnaire as Source-of-Truth for trustee answers.

4. **Cross-Story full-closure conditions** enumerated in `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md`. Stories needing post-Phase-0 substantive execution for full closure: 0.1 (AC-4 substitute engineer + full via 0.6); 0.2 (envelope-class dry-runs full via 1.10 + 1.5); 0.3 (AC-2 bus-factor switch-to-mirror full via 0.6); 0.4 (AC-1 comms-template ratification full via 0.13; AC-2 table-top full via 0.6); 0.5 (AC-3 comprehension administration full via 0.6 Task 10); 0.6 (AC-1 contract + IAM + onboarding via Task 9-11; AC-2 activation via Task 12); 0.7 (per-loop-node ratification per QA.2 disposition + 23-row backfill via 90-day substitute-bench window); 0.8/0.9/0.10/0.11 (recruitment + conduct + synthesis + reviewer-of-record sign-offs via Tasks 7-11); 0.13 (counsel selection + engagement-letter signature via Tasks 7-11); 0.14 (prototype build + measurement + ratify-or-pivot via Tasks 8-11); 0.15 (monthly review cadence + per-row closure events + Phase 1 launch readiness signal via Tasks 9-11).

5. **30-day takeover joint-discharge entry** per Decision 2026-06-05-032 (QC.1) fires when Stories 0.3 + 0.4 + 0.5 + 0.6 conditions all materialize. Solo Builder records the entry; ≥1-trustee acknowledgment via reviewer-of-record convention per QC.1 ratification.

6. **Phase-0 provisional closure does NOT retroactively change** any Phase-0 Story's `sprint-status.yaml` status. Status entries reflect framework-leg closure per author-commit. Substantive completion is tracked in `docs/launch-gate-inventory/inventory-roster.md` per Decision 2026-06-05-031 (Story 0.15 ratification).

7. **Critical-path priority after sign-off** per questionnaire lines 1009-1014: (1) Story 0.6 A-13 retainer authorization unblock execution (Decision 022); (2) Story 0.7 substitute-handler-bench start 2026-06-05 (Decision 023); (3) Story 0.13 counsel outreach kickoff (Decision 029); (4) Story 0.14 native-stack experiment kickoff (Decision 030); (5) Story 0.15 first monthly review 2026-07-01 (Decision 031); (6) Stories 0.8/0.9/0.10/0.11 empathy field-work execution per pre-recruitment approvals (Decisions 024-027).

**Open follow-ups:**

### Decision 2026-06-05-034: QC.3 ratified — sealing-mechanism ADR (Story 0.2) + code-escrow-mirror-destination ADR (Story 0.3) authored now; counsel-return-not-required; closes Story 0.2 Task 6 + Story 0.3 Task 7 ADR-legs fully

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — ADR-authoring timing.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of QC.3.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** QC.3 asked whether the credential-escrow-mechanism ADR (Story 0.2 Task 6) and the code-escrow-mirror-destination ADR (Story 0.3 Task 7) should be authored NOW or wait for Story 0.13 counsel return. Trustees confirmed (a) Author ADR now — the custodial-mechanism + mirror-destination decisions are settled per Decisions 2026-06-05-018 (sealing) + 2026-06-05-019 (mirror); they do NOT require legal counsel input.

**Decision body:**

1. **Credential-escrow-mechanism ADR — author now.** Solo Builder authors `docs/adr/ADR-NNNN-credential-escrow-mechanism.md` capturing: sealing mechanism = physical sealed envelopes in joint bank safe deposit (per Decision 018 / Q2.1); custodial location = trustee residences + bank safe deposit boxes (Q2.2); §2.10a control = separate custodial path per envelope class (Q2.3) — `prod-credential` envelopes at trustee residences; `audit-mirror-credential` envelopes at bank safe deposit; `high-sensitivity-tier-credential` envelopes at structurally distinct path per ADR specification; rotation/re-seal interface; dry-run repeatability; geographic-resilience property; §2.10a separability rationale (trustee-headcount = 3 per Q2.7 forces non-disjoint-subset mechanism; separate custodial path is the operative control).

2. **Code-escrow-mirror-destination ADR — author now.** Solo Builder authors `docs/adr/ADR-NNNN-code-escrow-mirror-destination.md` capturing: destination = GitLab.com under trustee-owned foundation account (per Decision 019 / Q3.1); credential model = SSH deploy key with push-only scope, stored as GitHub Actions secret `MIRROR_PUSH_CREDENTIAL` (Q3.2); branch protection enforced (no force-push; signed commits; ≥1 trustee approval per Q3.5); rotation cadence = annual (Q3.6); host-key pinning mechanism per Story 0.3 OQ 9 SSH TOFU resolution; geographic-resilience property (GitLab.com SaaS, cross-cloud independent of `asia-south1`).

3. **`docs/adr/` directory scaffolding.** Per Q3.3 ratified Story 0.3 scaffolds `docs/adr/` (template + index) as part of Task 7 ADR authoring. The scaffold lands concurrently with ADR-NNNN-credential-escrow-mechanism + ADR-NNNN-code-escrow-mirror-destination as the first two substantive ADRs. Story 0.15 architectural launch-gate inventory inventory-roster Row for ADR scaffolding flips from `open` to `closed`.

4. **Closure-language precision** per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`: Story 0.2 Task 6 + Story 0.3 Task 7 ADR-legs flip from `Resolved via explicit deferral pending ADR authoring` to `Closed by [edit]`. The substantive ADR landings replace the `[deferred ADR — placeholder procedure]` tags in `docs/escrow/sealing-procedure.md` §2 + `docs/escrow/code-escrow/mirror-procedure.md` §2 + `.github/workflows/code-escrow-mirror.yml` Step 2 inline comment.

5. **Counsel-input scope clarification.** This Decision establishes that custodial-mechanism + mirror-destination ADRs are NOT legal instruments requiring counsel return. Counsel return per Story 0.13 remains required for: comms-template ratification (Story 0.4 Task 9 × 5 templates); backup-engineer contract substantive language (Story 0.6 Task 9 §6 NDA + §9 Insurance + §10 Termination + §11 Dispute resolution); ADR-NNNN-threat-model-actor-inventory + ADR-NNNN-Cloudflare-pivot + ADR-NNNN-Hindi-native-trustee-ratification + ADR-NNNN-backup-engineer-contract-substantive-language + ADR-NNNN-engineer-identity-redaction-public-mirror (Story 0.5 ADR slot population × 5 — counsel-co-signed); DPO-breach-reporting envelope substantive content (Story 0.2 deferred row).

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder authors `docs/adr/` scaffolding + ADR-NNNN-credential-escrow-mechanism + ADR-NNNN-code-escrow-mirror-destination within the Decision 035 commit (or as a follow-up commit cited from this Decision).
- Story 0.2 sealing-procedure + Story 0.3 mirror-procedure + workflow YAML inline comment updates land in the same commit, removing `[deferred ADR — placeholder procedure]` tags.
- Story 0.15 inventory-roster row for ADR scaffolding flips to `closed` per Decision 031.

### Decision 2026-06-05-033: QC.2 ratified — annual re-attestation cadence start date 2027-07-01 for Story 0.5 KT pack + Story 0.15 launch-gate inventory; 12-month interval from Q15.3 first monthly review

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — Annual re-attestation cadence.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of QC.2.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** QC.2 set the annual re-attestation cadence start date for surfaces that require annual re-attestation: Story 0.5 KT pack (`docs/knowledge-transfer/kt-pack-ledger.md` "Annual re-attestation log") + Story 0.15 launch-gate inventory (`docs/launch-gate-inventory/engagement-ledger.md` §7 annual re-attestation log). Both surfaces commit annual re-attestation per their README §6 / README §6 review cadence fallbacks.

**Decision body:**

1. **Default annual re-attestation date = 2027-07-01.** Anchored to Q15.3 first monthly review date (2026-07-01) + 12 months. The 12-month interval applies symmetrically to both surfaces.

2. **Re-attestation scope per surface.** Story 0.5 KT pack: Trustee Panel walks the seven KT pack components + the comprehension questionnaire + the third-party-dependency-inventory + the ADR-slot status; confirms component freshness; identifies revision needs; records per-component re-attestation rows in `kt-pack-ledger.md`. Story 0.15 launch-gate inventory: Trustee Panel walks all 15 inventory-roster rows; confirms closed-row evidence still holds; accepts-risk rows still valid; deferred-per-named-criteria predicates still operative; reframed dispositions still consistent; records per-row annual re-attestation entries in `engagement-ledger.md §7`.

3. **Re-attestation triggers OUTSIDE annual cadence** remain operative per each Story's README §6: major-architecture-amendment triggers re-baseline; per-Epic estimation drift threshold triggers mid-cycle re-attestation; major-incident triggers ad-hoc re-attestation; explicit Trustee Panel discretion can convene a re-attestation at any time.

4. **First-cycle re-attestation 2027-07-01 anchors all subsequent cycles** at 2028-07-01, 2029-07-01, … unless superseded by a re-attestation-cadence amendment recorded as a new `.decision-log.md` `[CONTINUITY]` entry referencing this Decision.

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder appends placeholder rows in `kt-pack-ledger.md` "Annual re-attestation log" + `engagement-ledger.md §7` for the 2027-07-01 first cycle.
- Trustee Panel schedules the 2027-07-01 session at a reasonable lead time (recommended: ≥30 days advance notice per `monthly-review-cadence-protocol.md §4` precedent).
- Monthly review cadence per Decision 2026-06-05-031 (Story 0.15 Q15.3) continues monthly through the year; the annual re-attestation is additive, not a replacement.

### Decision 2026-06-05-032: QC.1 ratified — 30-day takeover joint-discharge entry recorded by Solo Builder when Stories 0.3+0.4+0.5+0.6 eight-condition union materializes; ≥1-trustee acknowledgment via reviewer-of-record convention

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — 30-day takeover joint-discharge convention.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of QC.1.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** QC.1 codified the convention for recording the 30-day takeover joint-discharge entry. Stories 0.3 + 0.4 + 0.5 + 0.6 each commit a follow-up `.decision-log.md` `[CONTINUITY]` entry recording the achievement when the eight-condition union closes:

> Story 0.3 AC-1 + AC-2 ∧ Story 0.4 AC-1 + AC-2 ∧ Story 0.5 AC-1 + AC-2 + AC-3 ∧ Story 0.6 AC-1 + AC-2

**Decision body:**

1. **Recording authority.** Solo Builder records the joint-discharge `.decision-log.md` `[CONTINUITY]` entry when all eight conditions materialize. The entry is authored as a single supersession referencing Decisions 2026-05-29-003 (Story 0.3) + 2026-05-29-004 (Story 0.4) + 2026-05-30-005 (Story 0.5) + 2026-05-30-006 (Story 0.6) + 2026-06-05-019 (Story 0.3 ratification) + 2026-06-05-020 (Story 0.4 ratification) + 2026-06-05-021 (Story 0.5 ratification) + 2026-06-05-022 (Story 0.6 ratification).

2. **Acknowledgment threshold = ≥1 trustee via reviewer-of-record convention.** Distinct from the ≥2-trustee co-sign discipline applied to individual Decision supersessions. Rationale: the joint-discharge entry is a *recording-of-achieved-state* event (each constituent Decision already carries its own trustee ratification), not a *new-decision* event. ≥1-trustee acknowledgment per the empathy-portfolio-reviewer-of-record convention (Decisions 008-011) is the operative threshold.

3. **Trustee-of-record nomination at recording time.** Solo Builder requests ≥1-trustee acknowledgment via the Trustee Panel chair on rota at the recording event. The acknowledging trustee records the date + ack signature in the entry. If no trustee is available, the entry is held in draft state until ≥1-trustee acknowledgment lands.

4. **Cross-link discipline.** The joint-discharge entry cross-links to: `docs/runbooks/operational-readiness-ledger.md` "30-day takeover joint-discharge" section; `docs/escrow/code-escrow/code-escrow-ledger.md` "Restoration drill + switch-to-mirror" entries; `docs/degradation-policy/degradation-policy-ledger.md` "Table-top exercise" entries; `docs/knowledge-transfer/kt-pack-ledger.md` "Comprehension administration" entries; `docs/backup-engineer/backup-engineer-ledger.md` "Activation event" entry.

5. **Expected timing per QX.2 = provisional Phase-0 closure.** Eight-condition union likely materializes in Q2/Q3 of Epic 1 timeframe (post-Story-0.6 backup-engineer table-top execution + post-Stories-1.10 / 1.5 substrate-dependent dry-runs). Solo Builder tracks progress in `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md` cross-Story coupling section.

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder monitors eight-condition union progress as Phase-0 stories advance from provisional to full closure.
- When the union materializes, Solo Builder authors the joint-discharge entry per the schema above and requests ≥1-trustee acknowledgment.

### Decision 2026-06-05-031: Story 0.15 Tasks 8-11 ratified — inventory-roster pack-as-a-unit + Rows 12-14 conditional-escalation-pending-predicate + first monthly review 2026-07-01 + counsel attendance coordinated via Story 0.13 + Phase 1 launch readiness framework confirmed; Rows 1+2 close concurrently per Q12.7 cross-link; supersession of Decision 2026-06-03-015 framework-leg

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q15.1-Q15.6.

Predicate materialization per architecture §Gap Analysis triggers per-row elevation to `open` then standard closure tracking per Task 10 discipline.

4. **Q15.4 — Legal Counsel meeting attendance.** Counsel attendance for Rows 3 (Edge/WAF DPDPA), 8 (DPDPA grievance officer), 9 (FR-43A external forum), 10 (Regulatory surface sign-off) coordinated via Story 0.13 engagement once counsel selection per Decision 2026-06-05-029 lands. Scope-relevance gating per `monthly-review-cadence-protocol.md §3`.

5. **Q15.5 — Phase 1 launch readiness signal framework confirmed.** Signal arms when every row at an architecture-allowed disposition (`closed` / `accepted-risk` / `deferred-per-named-criteria` / `reframed`) + objective evidence link per `closure-criteria-rubric.md §2` + no open-past-target rows without escalation review entry per `escalation-protocol.md §3`. Task 11 closure records the signal-armed event as a supersession on this Decision.

### Decision 2026-06-05-029: Story 0.13 Tasks 7-11 ratified — engagement-scope pack-as-a-unit + counsel-roster shortlist criteria confirmed + outreach paths (Trustee Panel network + Bar Council of Bihar + existing legal-counsel contacts) + ≥2 candidates required + standalone funding (Story 0.12 no-trigger) + engagement budget envelope ₹15k retainer / ₹7.5k standard / ₹15k expedited / ₹300k annual cap; supersession of Decision 2026-06-02-013 framework-leg

**Decision type:** Story 0.13 — Legal counsel engagement.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q13.1-Q13.7.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** Story 0.13 framework-leg author-committed via Decision 2026-06-02-013. Tasks 7-11 parked at `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION`. Trustees ratified Task 7 scope + Task 8 shortlist criteria + Task 9 budget disposition via Q13.1-Q13.7.

**Decision body:**

1. **Q13.1 — Engagement-scope ratification mode = pack-as-a-unit.** Scope items ratified as a single unit: (a) review-scope-charter §1 five AC items; (b) ~32-row cross-Story deferred-scope inventory; (c) regulatory-surface review; (d) ADR slot review; (e) pre-launch checkpoint coverage; (f) out-of-scope items; (g) counsel-roster shortlist criteria. Single ledger entry; this Decision is the supersession.

2. **Q13.2 — Counsel-roster shortlist criteria confirmed as drafted.** Mandatory: DPDPA practice; Indian Trust Act practice; concurrent-review-mode availability; no prior TSCT engagement; professional-indemnity insurance coverage. Preferred: financial-services regulatory; Bihar / Hindi context familiarity; mutual-aid trust track record.

3. **Q13.3 — Outreach paths = Trustee Panel professional network + Bar Council of Bihar referrals + existing legal-counsel contacts of board members.** Published-practice DPDPA experts in Hindi-language jurisdictions NOT in scope at this stage (may be added per subsequent supersession if first-pass shortlist yields fewer than ≥2 qualified candidates).

4. **Q13.4 — Single-candidate selection NOT permitted.** Strict ≥2 candidates per shortlist required; if first-pass yields fewer than ≥2 candidates meeting mandatory criteria, outreach paths expand per supersession entry (e.g., add Q13.3 option (d) published-practice DPDPA experts).

5. **Q13.5 — Funding cross-coupling resolved = separate dedicated legal-counsel funding allocation.** Story 0.12 reconciliation closed at no-trigger (no contract-help routing) per Decision 2026-06-04-016. Story 0.13 budget per Q13.6 is funded via a dedicated trust operating-budget line item (not standalone-fund per Story 0.12 contract-help-path-N/A). Decision 2026-06-02-013 body item 9 funding cross-coupling resolved.

6. **Q13.6 — Engagement budget envelope.** Retainer = ₹15,000/month (availability compensation; on-call regular access to counsel for clarifications + short-answer routine queries). Per-artifact standard pricing = ₹7,500/artifact (5-10 biz days return per `engagement-letter-template.md §4`). Per-artifact expedited pricing = ₹15,000/artifact (2-3 biz days surge). Annual cap = ₹300,000 (covers retainer ₹180k + up to ~16 standard artifacts ₹120k, OR fewer expedited artifacts; cap-breach triggers supersession + Trustee Panel re-ratification).

7. **Q13.7 — Emergency single-trustee scope-ratification fallback confirmed as drafted.** README §5 fallback valid under documented trustee incapacitation, time-bounded 30 days per Story 0.9 D-02 + Story 0.7 README §5 precedent; recorded as `.decision-log.md` `[LEGAL]` entry per supersession schema.

8. **Cross-Story unblock multiplier.** Story 0.13 Task 11 counsel returns unblock: Story 0.4 Task 9 comms-template ratification (×5 channels: push + WhatsApp + SMS + email + public-page-banner); Story 0.6 Task 9 contract template substantive language (§6 NDA + §9 Insurance + §10 Termination + §11 Dispute resolution); Story 0.5 ADR slot population × 5 (threat-model-actor-inventory + Cloudflare-pivot + Hindi-native-trustee-ratification + backup-engineer-contract-substantive-language + engineer-identity-redaction-public-mirror); Story 0.2 DPO-breach-reporting envelope substantive content. Cross-references per Decision 028 (Story 0.12 Task 9 ratification body item 7) funding-tradeoff resolution.

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder + Trustee Panel execute counsel shortlist outreach per Q13.3 paths within 30 days of this Decision (target outreach kickoff date 2026-06-15).
- Trustee Panel + Solo Builder conduct candidate interviews per Q13.4 ≥2-candidate discipline; selection per `engagement-ledger.md §4` Counsel-selection log.
- Engagement letter signature + NDA + COI disclosure (Task 9) within 60 days of selection.
- First review artifact (Epic 2 T&C draft) submitted to counsel within 2 weeks of signature per AC-1.
- Story 0.12 reconciliation backfill-log substantive backfill applied per Decision 028 body item 8.

### Decision 2026-06-05-027: Story 0.11 Tasks 7-11 ratified — host helpline institution = TSCT + pre-recruitment Trustee Panel approval (≥2-trustee both initialed) + Both trustees joint TERMINAL reviewer-of-record for P0-2 portfolio sign-off; supersession of Decision 2026-05-31-011 framework-leg

**Decision type:** Story 0.11 — P0-2d Operator shadowing.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q11.1-Q11.3.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1, initial `dr` on Q11.2), Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2, initial `kp` on Q11.2).

Trustees ratified Task 7 PRECONDITION-1 (host-institution choice) + PRECONDITION-2 (pre-recruitment panel approval) + Task 10 reviewer-of-record via Q11.1-Q11.3. Story 0.11 contributes the FOURTH and TERMINAL of four P0-2 legs.

**Decision body:**

1. **Q11.1 — Host helpline institution choice = TSCT.** TSCT selected per familiar context + established trustee relationship. Two-actor consent model — host TSCT leadership consents on operational-disruption + employee-participation + member-caller-privacy posture BEFORE individual operator approach. NSCT (sibling-trust) + analogous-trust paths NOT in scope at this stage; may be added per subsequent supersession if TSCT consent declined.

2. **Q11.2 — Pre-recruitment Trustee Panel approval = APPROVED (both trustees initial).** Approve the operator-shadowing exercise in principle, conditional on Q11.1 TSCT host-institution consent landing first. Approval covers: ≥4-hour shadowing across ≥2 distinct shifts; observer-not-participant discipline; per-call caller spoken-and-recorded consent; caller-revocation-mid-call discipline; emotional-overload-observer-discretion-end discipline. Both trustees initial (Trustee 1 = `dr`, Trustee 2 = `kp`) per Second Addendum sign-off block.

3. **Q11.3 — Reviewer-of-record = BOTH trustees joint TERMINAL sign-off.** Recommended given P0-2 portfolio-discharge weight. Both trustees co-review synthesis at `_bmad-output/research/p0-2d-operator-shadowing.md`; both sign off; Decision 2026-05-31-011 supersession authored as joint sign-off entry.

4. **P0-2 portfolio TERMINAL leg discharge trigger.** Task 11 closure (post Task 10 joint sign-off) discharges the **P0-2 four-leg portfolio (0.8 + 0.9 + 0.10 + 0.11) as TERMINAL** — final empathy-portfolio sign-off in Phase-0. A supersession entry on this Decision records the portfolio-TERMINAL discharge event with cross-references to Decisions 2026-06-05-024 (0.8) + 2026-06-05-025 (0.9) + 2026-06-05-026 (0.10).

5. **Task 7 execution sequencing.** Solo Builder approaches TSCT leadership for host-institution consent (PRECONDITION-1). If consent landed, Solo Builder recruits 1 operator via host-institution-mediated path (operator written consent obtained BEFORE shadowing begins). Per-call caller spoken-and-recorded consent obtained at the start of each observed call. Tasks 8-9 execution per `_bmad-output/research/p0-2d-operator-shadowing-protocol/`.

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder approaches TSCT leadership for host-institution consent within 14 days of this Decision; consent event logged in `host-helpline-engagement-log` with date + TSCT contact + consent terms + operational-disruption acknowledgment.

### Decision 2026-06-05-025: Story 0.9 Tasks 7-11 ratified — pre-recruitment Trustee Panel approval WITH CONSTRAINT (90-day-post-bereavement + voluntary + stop-anytime safeguard; ≥2-trustee both initialed) + Dhiraj facilitates trustee-mediated path via TSCT network + Both trustees panel-wide reviewer-of-record (Pattern 4 grammar evaluation); supersession of Decision 2026-05-31-009 framework-leg

**Decision type:** Story 0.9 — P0-2b Bereaved-spouse conversation.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q9.1-Q9.3.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1, initial `dr` on Q9.1), Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2, initial `kp` on Q9.1).

**Context.** Story 0.9 framework-leg author-committed via Decision 2026-05-31-009. Tasks 7-11 parked at `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION`. Trustees ratified Task 7 PRECONDITION (pre-conversation panel approval) + trustee-mediated recruitment path + Task 10 reviewer-of-record via Q9.1-Q9.3. Bereavement-context sensitivity escalates above 0.8's recruit-first model.

**Decision body:**

1. **Q9.1 — Pre-recruitment Trustee Panel approval = APPROVED WITH CONSTRAINT (both trustees initial).** Approve the bereaved-spouse conversation in principle subject to the following ethics safeguard: **Require trustee confirmation that the bereavement event occurred at least 90 days prior to recruitment and that participation is entirely voluntary with an explicit right to stop the conversation at any time.** Approval otherwise covers: ≥60-min Hindi conversation at spouse's chosen setting; re-consent-for-quotation discipline per ethics-protocol §2; Pattern 4 dignified-validation grammar evaluation. Both trustees initial (Trustee 1 = `dr`, Trustee 2 = `kp`) per Second Addendum sign-off block.

2. **Q9.2 — Trustee-mediated recruitment path = Dhiraj Rahul facilitates via TSCT network.** Solo Builder does NOT approach bereaved spouses directly; Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1) facilitates the introduction via TSCT network. Q9.1 constraint applies — Dhiraj confirms ≥90-day-post-bereavement criterion + voluntary-participation + stop-anytime-right at introduction time; confirmation recorded in `trustee-review-log` pre-conversation-approval row.

3. **Q9.3 — Reviewer-of-record = BOTH trustees panel-wide.** Recommended given Pattern 4 dignified-validation grammar evaluation downstream impact on UX spec §12 + Epic 6 (claim filing) + Epic 9 (NomineeConsole Sunita's surface) + Epic 11b (memorial) design freezes. Both trustees co-review synthesis at `_bmad-output/research/p0-2b-bereaved-spouse.md`; both sign off; Decision 2026-05-31-009 supersession authored as joint sign-off entry.

4. **Q9.1 constraint operationalization.** The 90-day-post-bereavement + voluntary + stop-anytime safeguard is recorded as a load-bearing structural property of the recruitment + conversation procedure. Operations: (a) Dhiraj's introduction confirms ≥90-day elapse from bereavement event; (b) introductory communication explicitly names voluntary participation with no consequence for declining; (c) opening conversational protocol restates the right to stop at any time without explanation or rationale required; (d) any indicator of distress triggers immediate conversation suspension per ethics-protocol §3. Recording in `host-engagement-log` per-event columns.

5. **Pattern 4 evaluation cross-coupling.** Synthesis Pattern 4 dignified-validation grammar evaluation per AC-1 + AC-2 outcome drives sample-copy revisions in UX spec §12 BEFORE Epic 6 design freeze; coordination with PRD-edit discipline per `[[feedback_architecture_vs_prd_boundary]]`.

**Open follow-ups:**

- Dhiraj Rahul facilitates TSCT-network introduction within 30 days of this Decision; introduction event logged in `trustee-review-log` pre-conversation-approval row with 90-day-criterion confirmation + voluntary-participation acknowledgment.
- Solo Builder conducts ≥60-min Hindi conversation at spouse's chosen setting per Task 8.
- Tasks 9-11 synthesis + joint trustee review + Pattern 4 revisions integrated into UX spec §12 before Epic 6 + Epic 9 + Epic 11b design freezes.
- If Dhiraj's TSCT path yields no candidate within 30 days: supersession entry expanding Q9.2 to Kalpana's network or joint facilitation.

### Decision 2026-06-05-023: Story 0.7 Tasks 8-11 ratified — substitute-handler-bench path (90-day window 2026-06-05 to 2026-09-03) + per-loop-node disposition table (4 BENCH + 3 BACKUP + 1 BACKLOG) + claim-filing as synthetic SLA test target; supersession of Decision 2026-05-30-007 framework-leg

**Decision type:** Story 0.7 — P0-1 Fallback-Handler Ledger.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q7.1 + Q7.3 + Q7.4 (via QA.2 per-loop-node table) + Q7.5.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1, initial `dr` on Q7.1), Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2, initial `kp` on Q7.1).

**Context.** Story 0.7 framework-leg author-committed via Decision 2026-05-30-007. Tasks 8-11 parked at `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION`. Story 0.12 reconciliation closed at no-trigger per Decision 028, so Operations Lead contract-help-path funding decision flowed to standalone disposition per Q7.1. Trustees ratified substitute-handler-bench path + per-loop-node dispositions + synthetic SLA test target via Q7.1 + Q7.3 + QA.2 + Q7.5.

**Decision body:**

1. **Q7.1 — Substitute-handler-bench path selected (NOT Operations Lead hire).** Trust elected temporary substitute-handler bench for the first 90 days to preserve capital during pre-launch validation. Operations Lead hiring deferred until operational load is evidenced by live member activity. Coverage provided by Trustee Panel plus backup-engineer framework once Story 0.6 Task 10 completes. AC-1 named-Operations-Lead-leg = **Resolved via explicit deferral** with rationale (substitute-bench is degraded-mode close, NOT UX-DR4 discharge).

2. **Q7.3 — Substitute-handler-bench composition + window.** Bench = Trustees Dhiraj Rahul + Kalpana Bharti. Story 0.6 backup engineer NOT in bench at this stage (gated on Story 0.6 Task 10 named-engineer landing). Named trustee-on-rota = Dhiraj Rahul. 90-day time-bound start date = 2026-06-05; end date = 2026-09-03 absent renewal. Renewal trigger: automatic review at Day 75 (2026-08-19). Renew only if Operations Lead remains unfilled AND Story 0.6 backup engineer has not assumed full operational coverage. UX-DR4 explicit-deferral rationale recorded in `operations-lead-commitment.md §4` substitute-handler-bench fallback log.

3. **Q7.4 / QA.2 — Per-loop-node disposition table.** Eight Phase-1 loop nodes assigned dispositions per QA.2 table:
   - **claim-filing → BENCH**: substitute-bench covers 90-day window with trustee-on-rota fallback; ratified now.
   - **peer-mesh → BACKLOG**: defer until post-launch operational evidence justifies substantive ratification.
   - **ground-inspection → BACKUP**: defer until Story 0.6 Task 10 closes (backup engineer contracted); ratify then.
   - **reconciliation → BENCH**: substitute-bench covers; ratified now.
   - **helpdesk → BENCH**: substitute-bench covers; tickets land naturally to trustees during 90-day window; ratified now.
   - **denial-appeal → BACKUP**: requires Niyamavali knowledge + counsel-touching; defer until Story 0.6 backup engineer + Story 0.13 counsel return; ratify then.
   - **kyc-fallback → BACKUP**: volume tied to DigiLocker failure rate; defer until Story 0.6 backup engineer; ratify then.
   - **upi-failure-coach → BENCH**: lightweight member coaching; trustee-on-rota acceptable; ratified now.
   
   Summary: 4 BENCH (claim-filing + reconciliation + helpdesk + upi-failure-coach) + 3 BACKUP (ground-inspection + denial-appeal + kyc-fallback) + 1 BACKLOG (peer-mesh).

4. **Substantive backfill of 23-row P0-1-pending placeholders.** For BENCH-dispositioned loop nodes (claim-filing + reconciliation + helpdesk + upi-failure-coach): `ledger.md §3` rows updated — `fallback_handler_role` flips from `<TO-BE-NAMED-BY-TRUSTEE-PANEL>` to substantive role names per substitute-bench composition (Trustee-Panel-on-rota with Dhiraj primary); `funding_status` = `volunteer-rota-bridge`; `status` = `trustee-signed-off-bench-disposition`. For BACKUP-dispositioned loop nodes (ground-inspection + denial-appeal + kyc-fallback): rows updated — `fallback_handler_role` = `<TO-BE-NAMED-POST-STORY-0.6-TASK-10>`; `funding_status` = `pending-backup-engineer-contracting`; `status` = `trustee-deferred-pending-backup-engineer`. For BACKLOG-dispositioned loop node (peer-mesh): row updated — `fallback_handler_role` = `<TO-BE-NAMED-POST-LAUNCH-EVIDENCE>`; `funding_status` = `pending-operational-evidence`; `status` = `trustee-deferred-pending-backlog`. Backfill-log applied per Story 0.7 Task 9 procedure.

5. **Q7.5 — Synthetic SLA test target = claim-filing.** Recommended default per AC-2 Given. Solo Builder schedules synthetic loop-node automation-failure SLA test per `ledger.md §6` test procedure with claim-filing as the target. Trustee Panel chair (Dhiraj Rahul) triggers; named fallback handler from BENCH disposition paged per published rota; SLA targets per claim-filing entry §11 escalation path.

6. **Closure-language precision.** Per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`: AC-1 funding-leg = Closed by [edit] for BENCH-dispositioned 4 nodes; Resolved via explicit deferral for BACKUP + BACKLOG dispositions. AC-1 named-role-leg = Closed by [edit] for BENCH (substitute-bench composition); Resolved via explicit deferral for BACKUP + BACKLOG. AC-1 rota-leg = Closed by [edit] for BENCH (rota populated with Dhiraj primary + Kalpana secondary); Resolved via explicit deferral otherwise. AC-2 synthetic SLA test = Resolved via explicit deferral pending Task 11 execution against claim-filing.

7. **UX-DR4 + AR-49 P0-1 Launch Gate Risks discharge.** Per AC-2 closure rule, UX-DR4 + AR-49 P0-1 row discharge triggers when AC-2 closes for ALL eight loop nodes (one synthetic test per node OR single multi-loop test). Per the BACKUP + BACKLOG dispositions, full discharge is deferred; partial discharge for BENCH-dispositioned 4 nodes via Task 11 execution against claim-filing initially, then reconciliation + helpdesk + upi-failure-coach across the 90-day window.

8. **90-day window expiry and renewal triggers.** At Day 75 (2026-08-19) automatic review per Q7.3 renewal trigger; at Day 90 (2026-09-03) absolute expiry absent renewal. Supersession entry on this Decision required for renewal or path-switch (e.g., to Operations Lead hire if substitute-bench inadequate or to expanded backup-engineer coverage if Story 0.6 Task 10 lands earlier).

**Open follow-ups:**

- Trustee Panel + Solo Builder schedule synthetic SLA test against claim-filing within 30 days of this Decision.
- Automatic Day-75 review scheduled for 2026-08-19; outcome recorded as supersession entry (renew / switch-to-Ops-Lead / extend-to-backup-engineer).

### Decision 2026-06-05-019: Story 0.3 Tasks 7-11 ratified — mirror destination = GitLab.com under trustee-owned foundation account + SSH deploy key credential model + branch protection enforced + annual rotation cadence + workflow-failure alerting deferred to Epic 1 Story 1.16x CI governance + Solo Builder provisional executor for restoration drill + bus-factor exercise (full closure via Story 0.6); supersession of Decision 2026-05-29-003 framework-leg

**Decision type:** Story 0.3 — Code Escrow.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q3.1-Q3.9.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** Story 0.3 framework-leg author-committed via Decision 2026-05-29-003. Tasks 7-11 parked at `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION`. Trustees ratified mirror destination + credential model + ADR scaffolding + release-branch set + branch protection + rotation cadence + alerting wiring + executor paths via Q3.1-Q3.9.

**Decision body:**

1. **Q3.1 — Mirror destination = GitLab.com under trustee-owned foundation account.** SaaS reliability + cross-cloud independent of `asia-south1` + established trustee account creation per GitLab.com hosting (region likely us-east; verify at provisioning time). `mirror-destination-inventory.md` Row 1 status flips `pending-ADR` → `provisioned-pending-wire` once trustee-owned account created; → `wired-pending-verification` post Task 8 secret wiring; → `verified` post Task 9 ≥2-trustee read-access verification.

2. **Q3.2 — Credential model = SSH deploy key** (push-only scope, stored as GitHub Actions secret `MIRROR_PUSH_CREDENTIAL`). Distinct from OAuth token / platform-native push-mirror / WIF analog candidates. Deploy key generated by Solo Builder per `mirror-procedure.md §1` Prerequisites; private key wired to GitHub Actions; public key registered at GitLab.com mirror destination per `access-grant-procedure` analog.

3. **Q3.3 — ADR directory scaffolding = scaffold in Story 0.3.** Per Q3.3 ratification, `docs/adr/` template + index + first ADR all in one event per Decision 034 (QC.3) authorization. Closes Story 0.2 Decision 002 Open Follow-up #6 (ADR directory scaffolding).

4. **Q3.4 — Release-branch set finalization = `main` + `release/*` patterns (post-Story-1.1).** Workflow currently mirrors only `main` (placeholder); Story 1.1 Turborepo bootstrap commits the branching model. Per Q3.4, expected model = release-cuts pattern `main` + `release/*`. Workflow YAML `on: push: branches:` list amendment per Story 0.3 OQ #1 supersession entry when Story 1.1 closes.

5. **Q3.5 — Mirror-destination branch protection ENFORCED.** No force-push; signed commits required; ≥1 trustee approval for write. Trustee-owned GitLab.com foundation account configures branch protection per Decision 034 ADR specification. Adds Task 7 ADR complexity but eliminates residual risk of leaked-credential-rewriting-mirror-history.

6. **Q3.6 — `MIRROR_PUSH_CREDENTIAL` rotation cadence = annual.** Per architecture §5.9 secret-rotation discipline. Rotation event logged in `code-escrow-ledger.md` "Workflow-secret-wiring log" supersession per cadence cycle; deploy key regenerated + GitLab.com public key updated + GitHub Actions secret updated.

7. **Q3.7 — Workflow-failure alerting wiring = defer to Epic 1 Story 1.16x CI governance.** The mirror workflow's on-failure step emits structured failure records but does NOT page on-call at Story 0.3 closure; Story 1.16x territory commits the alerting wiring. Story 0.3 OQ #4 closed with Story 1.16x ownership.

8. **Q3.8 — Restoration drill executor (Task 10) = Solo Builder provisional executor.** Story 0.6 backup engineer for full closure per Decision 022. Solo-Builder-executor closes AC-1 provisionally; full closure deferred to Story 0.6 Task 10 + Task 12 cross-coupling.

9. **Q3.9 — Bus-factor switch-to-mirror exercise (Task 11) executor = same path** (Solo Builder provisional; Story 0.6 backup engineer for full closure). AC-2 closure deferred to Story 0.6 backup engineer re-execution per Decision 022 cross-Story unblock.

10. **30-day-takeover joint-discharge contribution.** Per Decision 032 (QC.1 convention), this Decision's Tasks 9-11 closure contributes to the eight-condition union (Story 0.3 AC-1 + AC-2).

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder provisions trustee-owned GitLab.com foundation account within 14 days of this Decision; account creation event logged.
- Solo Builder generates SSH deploy key per Q3.2 + Task 8 wiring; secrets wired to GitHub Actions per `mirror-procedure.md §1` Prerequisites; mirror-push credential added to `docs/escrow/credential-inventory.md` per Story 0.2 inventory-row-addition sign-off lifecycle.
- Solo Builder triggers first release-branch push; Task 9 ≥2-trustee read-access verification within 7 days post-wiring.
- Solo Builder executes Task 10 restoration drill (provisional) within 30 days post-Task-9 closure; Task 11 bus-factor switch-to-mirror exercise within 30 days post-Task-10 (full closure deferred to Story 0.6 backup engineer per Q3.9).
- ADR-NNNN-code-escrow-mirror-destination authored per Decision 034; replaces `[deferred ADR — placeholder procedure]` tags in workflow YAML + procedures.

### Decision 2026-06-05-018: Story 0.2 Tasks 6-9 ratified — sealing mechanism = physical sealed envelopes in joint bank safe deposit + custodial locations = trustee residences + bank safe deposit boxes + §2.10a separation control = separate custodial path per envelope class + AC-2 dry-run-per-envelope-class amendment + audit-mirror envelope deferral to Story 1.10 + §5.9 reconciliation in ADR + trustee headcount = 3 + table-top via ≥2 trustees + substitute engineer; supersession of Decision 2026-05-29-002 framework-leg

**Decision type:** Story 0.2 — Credential Escrow.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q2.1-Q2.8.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** Story 0.2 framework-leg author-committed via Decision 2026-05-29-002. Tasks 6-9 parked at `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION`. Trustees ratified sealing mechanism + custodial location + §2.10a control + dry-run scope amendment + envelope deferral + §5.9 alignment + headcount confirmation + table-top execution path via Q2.1-Q2.8.

**Decision body:**

1. **Q2.1 — Sealing mechanism = physical sealed envelopes in joint bank safe deposit box.** Distinct from GPG-to-N-recipients / Shamir's Secret Sharing / password-manager-emergency-kit / hybrid candidates. Operational characteristics: rotation cadence per `secret-rotation.md`; dry-run repeatability via physical re-seal + verification check; geographic resilience via multiple bank locations; §2.10a separability via Q2.3 separate-custodial-path control.

2. **Q2.2 — Custodial location(s) = trustee residences + bank safe deposit boxes (≥2, geographically separated).** Multi-geographic distribution preserves geographic-resilience property. Notary/lawyer-held escrow NOT in scope at this stage; may be added per subsequent supersession if Story 0.13 legal counsel engagement (Decision 029) confirms feasibility.

3. **Q2.3 — §2.10a separation control = separate custodial path per envelope class.** Per Q2.7 trustee headcount = 3, strict disjoint subsets (option a) and constrained-overlap disjoint (option b) are unworkable; selected option (d) separate custodial path per envelope class. Operational: `prod-credential` envelopes at trustee residences; `audit-mirror-credential` envelopes at bank safe deposit (structurally distinct location); `high-sensitivity-tier-credential` envelopes at separate-path per ADR specification per Decision 034.

4. **Q2.4 — AC-2 dry-run-per-envelope-class amendment.** Original AC-2 = one dry-run on any non-load-bearing credential; amended to one rehearsal per envelope class (3 total). Full closure deferred to Stories 1.10 + 0.6 for audit-mirror + high-sensitivity classes per body item 5.

5. **Q2.5 — Audit-mirror envelope deferral accepted.** Framework accepts deferral of audit-mirror envelope instantiation until Story 1.10 (tamper-evident audit-log chain) closes; placeholder row in inventory tracks the gap.

6. **Q2.6 — High-sensitivity tier ↔ §5.9 alignment reconciliation = explicit reconciliation in ADR.** Per Decision 034 ADR specification, credential-escrow-mechanism ADR includes explicit reconciliation section addressing the two-actor distinction: operational engineers (architecture §5.9 two-person Terraform-mediated approval for rotation) vs. trustees (escrow framework ≥2-trustee quorum for opening). Reconciliation makes both pathways operative as distinct controls, not collapsed into one.

7. **Q2.7 — Trustee headcount = 3 confirmed.** PRD §11 statutory minimum satisfied. Disjoint-subset mechanisms (options a + b) unworkable at headcount = 3; fall back to Q2.3 (d) separate custodial path per envelope class. If trust expands to ≥4 trustees in future, supersession entry may re-evaluate options.

8. **Q2.8 — Bus-factor table-top execution = ≥2 trustees + table-top substitute engineer** (from Q1.3 per Decision 017). Solo Builder silent for the duration per `[[bus-factor simulation discipline]]`. AC-3 provisional closure path via ≥2 trustees + substitute; full closure deferred to Story 0.6 backup engineer per AC-3 closure rule.

### Decision 2026-06-05-017: Story 0.1 Tasks 4-5 ratified — runbook sign-off readiness across 7 runbooks + trustee-accessible repo definition = primary repo + Story 0.3 mirror + substitute engineer authorized (identity nominated by Trustee Panel) + re-sign threshold confirmed + §5.15 broader inventory ownership = Story 7.3 (cycle-freeze) + Story 10.3 (helpline operator escalation); supersession of Decision 2026-05-29-001 framework-leg

**Decision type:** Story 0.1 — Operational Runbooks.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q1.1-Q1.5.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** Story 0.1 framework-leg author-committed via Decision 2026-05-29-001 (which initialized `.decision-log.md` itself). Tasks 4-5 parked at `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION`. Trustees ratified per-runbook sign-off readiness + repo definition + substitute engineer authorization + re-sign threshold + §5.15 inventory ownership via Q1.1-Q1.5.

**Decision body:**

1. **Q1.1 — Per-runbook sign-off readiness = proceed.** Trustees proceed to ≥2-trustee sign-offs per runbook across all seven runbooks (14 signature events total): `deploy.md`, `rollback.md`, `secret-rotation.md`, `audit-log-integrity-verification.md`, `reconciliation-manual-intervention.md`, `rbac-seed-reset.md`, `multi-pariwar-provisioning.md`. Sign-off sessions scheduled post-Phase-0 closure.

2. **Q1.2 — Trustee-accessible repo definition = primary git repo + Story 0.3 mirror (GitLab.com per Decision 019).** Primary repo + mirror satisfies AC-2 "trustee-accessible" property. No separate trustee-only viewing surface required.

3. **Q1.3 — AC-4 substitute engineer authorization = APPROVED.** Table-top substitute engineer authorized for AC-4 provisional closure; identity to be nominated by Trustee Panel before execution (Trustee Panel owns substitute solicitation per Story 0.1 explicit clause — bus-factor simulation requires Solo Builder silent). Substitute-engineer execution closes AC-4 provisionally; full closure deferred to Story 0.6 contracted backup engineer re-execution per Decision 022 cross-Story unblock.

4. **Q1.4 — Re-sign threshold scheme confirmed as drafted.** AC-3 thresholds: ≥1 trustee for minor edits; ≥2 trustees for rollback procedure or contact escalation changes; higher governs on mixed edits.

5. **Q1.5 — §5.15 broader inventory ownership accepted.** Cycle-freeze operational procedure → Epic 7 Story 7.3 (candidate ownership confirmed); helpline operator escalation procedure → Epic 10 Story 10.3 (candidate ownership confirmed). Owning stories must commit runbook deliverables per AC; if Story 7.3 or Story 10.3 does not commit deliverable, follow-on story created at the relevant Epic per supersession entry.

6. **30-day-takeover joint-discharge contribution.** Per Decision 032 (QC.1 convention), this Decision's Task 5 closure (substitute-engineer execution) contributes to the eight-condition union via Story 0.1 AC-4 path 1 discharge (occurs via Story 0.6 Task 12 activation-scenario per Decision 022 cross-coupling).

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder coordinates ≥2-trustee sign-off sessions for the 7 runbooks within 30 days of this Decision; signature events logged in `operational-readiness-ledger.md` Sign-off log.
- Trustee Panel nominates AC-4 substitute engineer per Q1.3 substitute-solicitation clause; nomination event logged in operational-readiness-ledger.
- Solo Builder coordinates substitute-engineer execution of ≥1 runbook per topic in non-load-bearing target per AC-4; execution events logged in operational-readiness-ledger Execution-validation log.
- Story 0.6 backup engineer re-execution per Decision 022 Task 12 closes AC-4 path 1 fully.
- Story 7.3 + Story 10.3 deliverable commitments tracked per Story 0.15 inventory-roster per Decision 031.

### Decision 2026-06-04-016: Story 0.12 P0-3 spec-to-cadence reconciliation Task 8 no-trigger sign-off (`ceiling_ratio = 1.497 < 1.5`) — Epic 4 + Epic 12 medium-band reassignment rationale + 25→80 hr/week AI-assisted cadence override supersession + three-decision-paths enumeration (cut-scope N/A / move-SM-1 N/A / contract-help N/A); supersession of Decision 2026-06-01-012 + 2026-06-01-012-amend-1 framework-leg; ≥2-trustee acknowledgement pending Task 9 trustee panel session

**Decision type:** Story 0.12 — Spec-to-cadence reconciliation — cut-scope / move-SM-1 / contract-help decisions

**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed 2026-06-04 as part of `/bmad-code-review` Tasks 7+8 review closure (commit `aa6238d` reviewed; 23 unified findings triaged: 5 decisions resolved + 20 patches applied + 2 deferred). Tasks 9-11 explicitly tagged `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION_` per Story 0.12 scope discipline.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), claude-opus-4-7 bmad-code-review execution.

**Ratifying trustees:** None at author-commit; **≥2-trustee acknowledgement slot pending Task 9** trustee panel session per `reconciliation-decision-framework.md §4` (no-trigger ratification follows the same ≥2-trustee discipline as trigger-fired ratification; supersession-marker for Decision 2026-06-01-012-amend-1 applies).

**Context.** Commit `aa6238d` ("Story 0.12 Tasks 7+8 — substantive engineer-month estimates + SM-1 mismatch-ratio") landed substantive engineer-month estimates across 8 per-loop-node + 3 per-tier + 15 epic-aggregation worksheet rows at AI-assisted 80 hr/week cadence; computed `total_estimate_floor = 4.73 months`, `total_estimate_ceiling = 13.47 months`; computed `floor_ratio = 0.788`, `ceiling_ratio = 1.497`; `max(0.788, 1.497) = 1.497 < 1.5` per the strict-`>` threshold → reconciliation trigger does NOT fire. The `/bmad-code-review 0.12` Tasks 7+8 review pass surfaced three load-bearing levers keeping the ceiling under 1.5 — Epic 4 confidence-band upgraded `low → medium` (commit body admitted "critical to keeping ceiling_ratio below 1.5"), Epic 12 retained `medium` despite TBD FR-43A platform substrate, and the 25→80 hr/week cadence shift was asserted in 11 entry files without methodology amendment. BigDev resolved the five decision-needed items at the Tasks 7+8 review checkpoint: D-01 keep Epic 4 `medium` with §3-amendment + per-epic rationale + supersession entry (this Decision); D-02 keep Epic 12 `medium` with per-epic rationale + supersession entry (this Decision); D-03 formalize 25→80 hr/week cadence override via methodology amendment + supersession entry (this Decision); D-04 → defer (W-02) — accept strict-`>` reading without §1.bis proximity carve-out (Month-3 re-attestation absorbs); D-05 collapse worksheet §8 reconciliation_decision_proposal cell + author three-decision-paths enumeration here. The composite scope of D-01 + D-02 + D-03 + D-05 maps onto a single supersession entry on Decision 2026-06-01-012-amend-1; this Decision is that entry.

**Decision body:**

1. **No-trigger outcome at Task 8.** Computed mismatch ratios reported at full precision per Tasks 7+8 review P-01: `floor_ratio = 0.788` (4.73 ÷ 6 = 0.7883…); `ceiling_ratio = 1.497` (13.47 ÷ 9 = 1.4967…). `max(0.788, 1.497) = 1.497`. Strict-`>` 1.5 threshold not crossed. Reconciliation-decision step optional per `reconciliation-decision-framework.md §1` no-trigger record; this Decision is the AR-49 P0-3 discharge-pending sign-off artifact even at no-trigger. The 0.003 margin is acknowledged as load-bearing and deferred to Month-3 re-attestation per W-02 (no §1.bis proximity carve-out added at this commit).

2. **Three-decision-paths enumeration (per D-05-resolved Tasks 7+8 review).** Each of the `reconciliation-decision-framework.md §2` taxonomy paths is explicitly enumerated and recorded as `N/A — no trigger fired`:
   - **(a) Cut-scope** — `N/A — no trigger fired`. No Stories deferred to v2 / Phase-2 / future-amendment. `deferred-work.md` "## Story 0.12 reconciliation deferrals" section not opened. Sprint-status.yaml `development_status` entries unchanged. Epic List unchanged.
   - **(b) Move-SM-1** — `N/A — no trigger fired`. PRD §7 SM-1 6-9 month target unchanged. No `ADR-NNNN-SM-1-amendment` authored. `reconciliation-decision-framework.md §3(b)` move-SM-1 procedure not invoked.
   - **(c) Contract-help** — `N/A — no trigger fired`. No scope outsourced to external contractors. Trustee operating budget unchanged with respect to spec-to-cadence reconciliation (Story 0.13 legal counsel engagement budget + Story 0.14 prototype device-procurement budget remain governed by their respective Story decisions; no Story 0.12 contract-help allocation).

3. **Epic 4 Rules Engine `low → medium` band reassignment rationale (per D-01-resolved Tasks 7+8 review).** Cited in full at `docs/spec-to-cadence-reconciliation/per-epic-aggregation-estimates/epic-4.md §6`. Summary: `estimation-methodology.md §3-amendment` AI-cadence band reassignment sub-rule (added 2026-06-04 per this Decision) permits reassignment when all three criteria hold — (a) **specification completeness**: PRD §4.2 FR-7 through FR-12A fully specifies Rules Engine functional requirements with no remaining "TBD" gaps; (b) **AI prior-art availability**: extensive AI training-data exposure to expression-tree evaluators, decision tables, RETE-pattern matchers, JSON-schema validators, config DSLs; (c) **substrate-adjacency**: Rules Engine is adjacent to JSON-schema validators + config DSLs + decision tables (novel to TWT, mature in open-source ecosystem). Reassignment-margin transparency: `low` band would yield Epic 4 floor 0.21 / ceiling 0.84 (vs `medium` floor 0.28 / ceiling 0.63); replacing 0.84 → 0.63 reduces `total_estimate_ceiling` by 0.21 (13.68 → 13.47), shifting `ceiling_ratio` 1.520 → 1.497, the load-bearing trigger-margin determinant. Trustees evaluate on three-criteria merits, not on trigger-margin consequence; rejection at Task 9 fires the trigger and invokes the three-decision-paths taxonomy.

4. **Epic 12 Module Marketplace `medium` band rationale despite TBD FR-43A platform (per D-02-resolved Tasks 7+8 review).** Cited in full at `docs/spec-to-cadence-reconciliation/per-epic-aggregation-estimates/epic-12.md §6`. Summary: AI-cadence offsets the ±30% surface-count uncertainty from the TBD external-forum platform decision via three mechanisms — (1) **substrate-pivot velocity at AI-cadence** (per-platform adapter swap is a 1-2 day exercise post-decision); (2) **surface-count delta absorbed within medium-band ceiling** (±30% on midpoint 0.18 → [0.126, 0.234]; medium-band ceiling 0.27 covers the upper bound with margin); (3) **forum-platform choice is bounded** (which-of-4 candidates: Discourse / Flarum / Lemmy / hand-rolled — structurally different from unbounded substrate-unknown cases). Reassignment-margin transparency: `low` band would yield Epic 12 floor 0.09 / ceiling 0.36 (vs `medium` floor 0.12 / ceiling 0.27); replacing 0.36 → 0.27 reduces `total_estimate_ceiling` by 0.09 (13.56 → 13.47), shifting `ceiling_ratio` 1.507 → 1.497. **Combined with D-01-resolved**: reverting EITHER Epic 4 or Epic 12 to `low` fires the trigger; reverting BOTH fires more decisively (ratio ≈ 1.530). Month-3 re-attestation will re-baseline Epic 12 against the actual platform choice per `README.md §6` mid-cycle drift escalation rule.

5. **25 → 80 hr/week AI-assisted cadence override supersession (per D-03-resolved Tasks 7+8 review).** `estimation-methodology.md §2 row 1` (solo cadence: 25 hr/week NET) preserved as the documented default; **§2 row 2 added 2026-06-04 per this Decision** to codify AI-assisted cadence (80 hr/week NET) as the operational override with explicit per-month basis (`1 AI-cadence month = 346 hr`; 80 × 52 / 12 = 346.67 floored). `§5 Assumption catalogue` row 1 override-trigger column extended with "AI-assisted cadence amendment 2026-06-04" criterion. `README.md §10` Engineer-month glossary entry updated to enumerate both cadence rows. Supporting evidence catalogue: Phase-0 framework-authoring throughput measurement (15 stories author-committed in 5 calendar days; see `per-loop-node-estimates/claim-filing.md §5`). The 2× implementation-vs-Phase-0 difficulty premium baked into the 80 hr/week basis is itself an unsourced assumption — flagged as Month-3 re-attestation re-evaluation target (actual ratio may be 3-5×; if so, this entire basis is overoptimistic). All 11 entry-file §5 derivations now cite `methodology §2 row 2` as the cadence-basis authority rather than asserting 80 hr/week free-floating.

6. **Cadence-override ratification cross-coupling.** The cadence override (item 5) is itself a substantive change to the methodology that requires ≥2-trustee co-sign per `reconciliation-decision-framework.md §4` PRD-edit-discipline analog. Ratification of THIS Decision (Decision 2026-06-04-016) at Task 9 trustee panel session simultaneously ratifies (a) the no-trigger outcome (item 1); (b) the three-decision-paths enumeration (item 2); (c) the Epic 4 + Epic 12 band reassignments (items 3 + 4); (d) the cadence-override supersession (item 5). All five are bundled per the bundled-supersession discipline; trustees may reject any subset, in which case the rejected items revert to their pre-Decision state and downstream cascading effects (e.g., trigger-firing) follow.

7. **Funding-tradeoff cross-references status (per Tasks 7+8 review P-06).** This no-trigger outcome does NOT resolve the funding-tradeoff cross-references parked in upstream framework artifacts; they remain at `citation-slot-committed` in `backfill-log.md`. The substantive resolution is deferred to Task 9 trustee ratification of: (a) Story 0.6 A-13 backup-engineer retainer sizing (no trigger means no contract-help allocation, so the A-13 retainer remains at the Decision 2026-05-30-006 framework-leg-only state); (b) Story 0.7 Operations Lead salary funding source (no trigger means no Operations Lead contract-help allocation, so the salary-range remains at the Decision 2026-05-30-007 deferred-pending state); (c) per-loop-node fallback-handler funding posture × 8 per `docs/fallback-handler-ledger/ledger.md §3` (no trigger means no per-loop-node retainer-vs-salary-vs-volunteer-rota-bridge decisions are triggered by Story 0.12; they remain in their respective Story 0.7 author-commit deferred-pending states). Story 0.13 legal counsel engagement budget + Story 0.14 prototype device-procurement budget are governed by their respective Decisions (2026-06-02-013 + 2026-06-02-014).

8. **Threshold-proximity deferral (per D-04-resolved Tasks 7+8 review → W-02).** No `reconciliation-decision-framework.md §1.bis` proximity carve-out added at this Decision. The 0.003-margin concern is real but defer-to-Month-3-re-attestation is the operative response: Month-3 re-attestation will re-baseline the low-confidence Epics (E6 / E7 / E9) against actual build velocity and surface any true threshold breach with empirical evidence rather than speculative band-internal safety rules. If Month-3 re-attestation shows `ceiling_ratio` drifting toward 1.5 from above-or-below, this Decision is re-opened via supersession entry and the §1.bis carve-out may be revisited at that point.

9. **Supersession-schema marker.** This Decision supersedes Decision 2026-06-01-012-amend-1 with respect to: (a) Epic 4 confidence-band methodology classification (Epic 4 moves from "canonical low example" to "low at solo cadence; medium at AI-cadence"); (b) cadence assumption row 1 default (extended with row 2 AI-assisted override); (c) §3 confidence-band sub-rules (added §3-amendment AI-cadence band reassignment criteria); (d) no-trigger outcome (newly authored — was pending Task 8 closure at Decision 012-amend-1 close). It does NOT supersede with respect to: backfill-log inventory (19 rows holds); §3(d) hybrid-as-composition-pattern reframing; §8 §7-as-deterministic-source rule; methodology §4(b) per-surface cap; per-tier §1-§8 schema extension; asymmetric geometric formula; SM-1 in-scope-of-architecture-Decision-Freeze + ADR-NNNN-SM-1-amendment PRD-edit precondition; cut-scope cascade-dependency invariant; anti-laundering ≥2-distinct-trustee rule; day-30 reversal + quorum-unavailable fallbacks; backfill-log source_line drift recovery; re-attestation major-architecture-amendment threshold + mid-cycle drift escalation; engineer-month context-switching deduction de-duplication. All non-superseded provisions of Decision 012 + 012-amend-1 remain in force.

10. **Story 0.12 sprint-status flipped done → review per closure-language-precision discipline.** Per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: framework-leg (Tasks 1-6) = Closed by [edit]; substantive-estimate-leg + Task 8 mismatch-ratio leg = Closed by [edit]; Tasks 9-11 legs (≥2-trustee ratification + Epic List + sprint plan substantive updates + Step 4 final validation + AR-49 P0-3 discharge evidence file) = Resolved via explicit deferral pending external action. `sprint-status.yaml:98` flipped `0-12-p0-3-spec-to-cadence-reality-check-reconciled: done` → `review` per Tasks 7+8 review P-04.

**Open follow-ups:**

- **Task 9 — Trustee Panel ≥2-trustee acknowledgement** of this composite Decision (items 1-10 bundled per item 6 ratification cross-coupling). Supersession-schema marker on Decision 2026-06-01-012-amend-1; per-item rejection permitted (rejection of any single item invokes downstream cascading effects, e.g., rejection of items 3 OR 4 fires the trigger).
- **Task 10 — Sprint-plan + Epic List substantive edits** required only if Task 9 ratification rejects any of items 3, 4, 5 (each rejection triggers the three-decision-paths taxonomy). If Task 9 ratifies as-bundled: sprint-status.yaml + epics.md require no substantive edits (no scope cuts; no SM-1 move; no contract-help allocation). The `development_status` flip done → review at item 10 is unrelated to scope edits.
- **Task 11 — Step 4 final validation** via `bmad-check-implementation-readiness` against the (unchanged) reconciled scope; report at `implementation-readiness-report-post-reconciliation-2026-XX-XX.md`; AR-49 P0-3 discharge evidence file authored as supersession entry on this Decision per `reconciliation-decision-framework.md §7`.

**Decision body:**

1. **`docs/launch-gate-inventory/` canonical location** as new top-level surface parallel to the ten existing framework surfaces (`runbooks/` + `escrow/` + `degradation-policy/` + `knowledge-transfer/` + `adr/` + `backup-engineer/` + `fallback-handler-ledger/` + `spec-to-cadence-reconciliation/` + `legal-counsel-engagement/` + `native-stack-validation/`).

2. **Seven component artifacts** inheriting Story 0.1-0.7 + 0.12 + 0.13 + 0.14 framework-as-top-level-surface patterns:
   - `README.md` (11 sections with 14 structural invariants + disjoint anchor §9 establishing Story 0.15 as the SEVENTH Phase-0 portfolio + 30+ domain glossary terms §10)
   - `inventory-roster.md` (15 rows: 12 architecture §Launch Gate Risks verbatim from architecture lines 4778-4788 + 3 conditional-escalation candidates from architecture §Gap Analysis lines 4802-4815 + 4817-4840 + 1 reserved); append-only + supersession-only lifecycle inherited from Stories 0.3/0.4/0.5/0.6/0.7/0.12/0.13/0.14
   - `closure-criteria-rubric.md` (§1 Authority cites + §2 Objective evidence vocabulary 11 artifact types + §3 Testable signal vocabulary 5 types + §4 Weak-closure-criteria rejection examples 8 patterns + §5 Per-disposition closure-evidence requirements + §6 Multi-link rows + §7 Cross-links)
   - `target-date-rationale-template.md` (§1 Authority cites + §2 Per-row rationale schema + §3 Date format conventions with relative-to-fact triggers preferred per [[feedback_architecture_vs_adr_boundary]] + §4 Sequenced gates pattern + §5 Decomposed gates pattern + §6 Conditional candidates pattern + §7 Annual re-attestation + §8 Worked examples for Rows 1/7/11/12 + §9 Cross-links)
   - `monthly-review-cadence-protocol.md` (§1 Authority cites + §2 Meeting agenda template 6 items + §3 Quorum ≥2-trustee + §4 Cadence monthly/quarterly-fallback/annual + §5 Meeting-minutes schema + §6 Emergency review triggers 4 types + §7 Annual re-attestation walk-through + §8 Cross-links)
   - `escalation-protocol.md` (§1 Escalation triggers 5 types + §2 Escalation review process + §3 Escalation outcomes vocabulary 5 outcomes + §4 Escalation log row schema + §5 Repeat-escalation root-cause inquiry trigger + §6 Phase 1 launch readiness signal + §7 Cross-links)
   - `engagement-ledger.md` (§1 Header + §2 Lifecycle definition + §9 P0-N divergence reconciliation log + §10 Pack-revision log + §11 Cross-links to ten related framework ledgers substantively populated at Task 7 author-commit; §3-§8 carry `<PENDING-TASK-N>` placeholders for the lifecycle log entries that land at Tasks 8-11)

3. **AR-49 + epics line 689 + PRD §12 Phase 0 line 1467 + Sprint Change Proposal Item 17 + architecture §Launch Gate Risks closure path committed** — author-commit closes the framework-leg; Tasks 8-11 closure discharges the AR-49 commitment + epics line 689 Epic 0 Deliverable + PRD §12 Phase 0 line 1467 architectural-launch-blocker-gates bullet substantively backed + Sprint Change Proposal Item 17 EDIT 17A substantively backed + architecture §Launch Gate Risks per-row closure-or-disposition + Phase 1 launch readiness signal armed when all-rows-closed-or-deferred.

4. **Inventory-roster is 15 rows** — 12 architecture verbatim (Rows 1-11 from architecture lines 4778-4788; note Rows 1-11 number 11 of the 12-row architecture table because architecture row order maps 1:1 to inventory rows 1-11) + 3 Gap Analysis conditional-escalation candidates (Rows 12-14 from architecture lines 4802-4815 + 4817-4840) + 1 reserved (Row 15 for post-author-commit conditional-escalation elevation). All architecture row labels cited verbatim per [[feedback_architecture_vs_prd_boundary]] (architecture remains source-of-truth for row labels).

5. **Trustee threshold is ≥2-trustee uniform** across inventory-ratification (Task 8) + per-row-closure (Task 10) + final-sign-off (Task 11) — distinct from Story 0.14's ≥1-trustee BigDev-decision-authority pattern. Re-litigation post-Epic-1 launch of a closed gate requires the same ≥2-trustee threshold; per-row closure cannot land under single-trustee fallback even for low-stakes rows.

The architecture P0-4 (UX-deliverable empty/skeleton/error-state inventory) is a downstream Epic 1 or Epic 11a deliverable, NOT discharged by Story 0.13. Divergence is documented in Row 6 `notes` + `engagement-ledger.md` §9 P0-N divergence reconciliation log; do not patch architecture from a Story.

8. **Gap Analysis conditional-escalation observations tracked as conditional-escalation-pending-predicate per [[feedback_gap_analysis_observational]]** — three observations (Feature-flag tool selection P1 at architecture lines 4817-4828; FR-20 pool-spawn capacity envelope at lines 4830-4840; Composed Account State enumeration at lines 4802-4815) elevate to Launch Gate Risks ONLY when their verbatim predicate materializes; pre-emptive elevation is forbidden. Each row carries the verbatim predicate text in `closure_criteria`.

### Decision 2026-06-02-013: P0-4 Legal Counsel Concurrent-Review Engagement framework authored at docs/legal-counsel-engagement/ + engagement-letter-template skeleton + review-scope-charter with 5 AC-named scope items + 32-row cross-Story deferred-scope inventory + 13-row regulatory surface review + 6-row pre-launch checkpoint coverage + review-artifact-roster with 19 priority-ordered placeholder rows + per-artifact-return-roster mirroring schema + counsel-roster with shortlist criteria + single template row at pending-trustee-selection + engagement-ledger with 11 §-log sections; Trustee Panel scope ratification + counsel selection + engagement-letter signature + first-artifact submission + counsel returns + Epic 2/3/6 integration pending

**Decision type:** Story 0.13 — Legal Counsel Engagement

**Status:** Author-committed 2026-06-02 as part of `/bmad-dev-story` Story 0.13 author-commit run. Tasks 1-6 (framework authoring) closed; Tasks 7-11 (Trustee Panel scope ratification + counsel selection + engagement-letter signature + first-artifact submission + counsel returns + Epic 2/3/6 integration) explicitly tagged `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION_` per Story 0.13 scope discipline.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), claude-opus-4-7 dev-story execution

**Ratifying trustees:** None at author-commit; ≥2-trustee scope ratification pending Task 7 + signed engagement letter Task 9.

**Context.** Story 0.13 of Epic 0 commits the P0-4 legal counsel concurrent-review engagement framework per UX §Phase-0 P0-4 launch-blocker (UX spec line 109: "Legal counsel onboarded with concurrent-review scope before §1 Trust Loops drafting begins. Scope includes: trust-posture copy review during drafting (not after); DPDPA consent flow design review; denial-appeal flow procedural-fairness review; Account State Machine transition-table review for notice/service formalities; dual-path claim authority-to-file evidentiary specification. Owner: Trustee Panel (engagement) + Legal Counsel (review)") + epics line 564 + 687 Phase-0 prereq gate P0-4 + architecture §External Validation Pending (architecture lines 4842-4860) + architecture §Launch Gate Risks subsidiary legal-counsel-naming rows (architecture lines 4785-4788: DPDPA grievance officer designation + FR-43A external forum destination + Regulatory surface sign-off + Trust formation + legal registration) + PRD §4.14.1 regulatory surface inventory (PRD line 1169 "Canonical; legal counsel must sign off pre-launch") + PRD §10.1 trust-posture legal caveat (UX spec line 75 "Legal counsel is engaged from spec-drafting forward, with concurrent-review scope; their findings shape the spec, not just check it"). Story 0.13 constitutes the **FIFTH Phase-0 portfolio** distinct from bus-factor-of-one mitigation (Stories 0.1-0.6) + loop-node operational-responsiveness (Story 0.7) + empathy field-work (Stories 0.8-0.11) + spec-to-cadence-funding-reconciliation (Story 0.12); Story 0.13 closure events discharge the UX §Phase-0 P0-4 launch-blocker + the epics line 564 + 687 P0-4 launch-gate property + the architecture §Launch Gate Risks subsidiary legal-counsel-naming rows + are one of four leg discharges (P0-1 = Story 0.7; P0-3 = Story 0.12; P0-4 = Story 0.13; P0-5 = Story 0.14) all of which must close before Epic 1 substrate work begins.

**Decision body:**

1. **`docs/legal-counsel-engagement/` canonical location** as new top-level surface parallel to the eight existing framework surfaces (`runbooks/` + `escrow/` + `degradation-policy/` + `knowledge-transfer/` + `adr/` + `backup-engineer/` + `fallback-handler-ledger/` + `spec-to-cadence-reconciliation/`).

2. **Seven component artifacts** inheriting Story 0.1-0.7 + 0.12 framework-as-top-level-surface patterns:
   - `README.md` (11 sections with ≥13 structural invariants + disjoint anchor §9 + domain glossary §10)
   - `engagement-letter-template.md` (14-section framework skeleton with `<COUNSEL-RETURN PLACEHOLDER>` markers in §7 NDA + §10 Work-product ownership + §11 SLA breach + §12 Insurance + §13 Termination)
   - `review-scope-charter.md` (8 sections: §1 five AC-named primary scope items + §2-§3 ~32-row cross-Story deferred-scope inventory + §4 13-row regulatory surface review + §5 ADR slot review + §6 6-row pre-launch checkpoints + §7 out-of-scope items + §8 charter signature path)
   - `review-artifact-roster.md` (19 priority-ordered placeholder rows: 5 priority-1-onwards AC-named scope items + 14 priority-6-onwards cross-Story deferred-scope rows + multi-Pariwar)
   - `per-artifact-return-roster.md` (19 placeholder rows mirroring review-artifact-roster + return-content schema extension: `return_summary` + `return_substantive_changes_required` + `return_open_questions` + `return_next_review_cycle` + `integration_pr_or_commit_ref` + `integration_validation_outcome` + `supersession_marker`)
   - `counsel-roster.md` (shortlist criteria + single template row at `pending-trustee-selection` status + multi-counsel pattern documentation)
   - `engagement-ledger.md` (11 §-log sections: §1 Header + §2 Lifecycle + §3 Trustee scope ratification log + §4 Counsel-selection log + §5 Engagement-signature log + §6 First-artifact-submission log + §7 Return-receipt log + §8 SLA-breach-tracking log + §9 Periodic re-attestation log + §10 Pack-revision log + §11 Cross-links to related framework ledgers + open-questions ledger)

3. **UX §Phase-0 P0-4 + epics line 564 + 687 + architecture §External Validation Pending Legal counsel closure path committed** — author-commit closes the framework-leg; Task 7 closure (Trustee Panel scope ratification) + Task 8 closure (counsel selection) + Task 9 closure (engagement-letter signature + NDA + COI disclosure) + Task 10 closure (first-artifact submission within 2 weeks of signing) + Task 11 closure (counsel returns + Epic 2/3/6 integration) discharges UX §Phase-0 P0-4 + epics line 564 + 687 P0-4 launch-blocker + architecture §Launch Gate Risks subsidiary legal-counsel-naming rows at architecture lines 4785-4788.

4. **Cross-Story upstream framework artifact pending-legal-review-return event-receiver committed** — the eleven upstream framework artifacts' (Stories 0.2 + 0.4 + 0.5 + 0.6 + 0.7 + 0.12) pending-counsel-return state gains a coherent legal-review forum routing through Story 0.13; 32 rows in `review-scope-charter.md §3` cross-reference the upstream sources. Inventory total at author-commit verified per dev-time grep: 146 raw "Story 0.13" / "PENDING LEGAL REVIEW" matches across `docs/` (Story 0.13 framework files excluded) consolidated to 32 distinct artifact-roster targets — within the ±5 variance allowance per AC-1 + Open Question #5.

5. **Concurrent-review nature is load-bearing structural property** — counsel reviews artifacts during drafting and pre-launch per UX spec line 75 "their findings shape the spec, not just check it"; NOT post-hoc audit. Committed in `README.md` §4 invariant 1 + `engagement-letter-template.md` §2 + §9. The concurrent-review-mode availability is the load-bearing engagement-term property — counsel may not decline availability across the term without breach.

6. **Counsel-roster supports multiple counsel** — multi-counsel engagement permitted if practice-area scope exceeds single counsel competence (DPDPA + Indian Trust Act + financial-services may be split across 2-3 counsel); the framework does not assume one. Multi-counsel coordination pattern documented in `counsel-roster.md` with deferred ADR per `adr-index.md` Section K row #8.

7. **Self-referencing engagement-letter substantive-language dependency is intentional** — the engagement-letter substantive language (§7 NDA + §10 Work-product + §11 SLA + §12 Insurance + §13 Termination + jurisdiction clauses + dispute resolution + indemnification + force majeure + governing law) is provided by the named counsel selected at Task 8 (the same counsel who will execute the engagement); this is unlike Story 0.6 contract-template which depends on Story 0.13 closure; here, the same counsel reviews and edits the engagement letter for their own engagement, then signs. This is a known regulatory pattern for self-engaging counsel; documented as a structural-invariant per `README.md` §4 invariant 13 + Open Question #4.

8. **First-artifact-within-2-weeks-of-signing is a structural property** — AC-1 commits this as a deadline; the post-signature window is the operational-readiness clock; the first artifact is Epic 2 T&C draft per `review-artifact-roster.md` Row 1 priority-1 (artifact_id `epic-2-tc-draft-v1`). Definition is calendar-day weeks (14 calendar days), NOT business-week (10 business days) per Open Question #9 — this is more conservative + matches the AC-1 "within 2 weeks of signing" plain wording.

9. **Story 0.12 P0-3 spec-to-cadence reconciliation contract-help-path budget cross-coupling resolution** — the contract-help-path budget reservation for legal counsel concurrent-review scope per `docs/spec-to-cadence-reconciliation/reconciliation-decision-framework.md` §3(c) + Decision 2026-06-01-012 body item 9 gains substantive resolution at Story 0.13 Task 9 engagement-signature event; the substantive retainer + per-artifact pricing is recorded in the engagement letter §5 + cross-referenced from `docs/spec-to-cadence-reconciliation/backfill-log.md` (Story 0.12 backfill row flipped from `citation-slot-committed` to `substantive-backfill-applied` at Task 9).

10. **P0-N numbering divergence flagged as Open Question #2** — architecture's AR-49 P0-4 row at line 4783 names "P0-4 Empty/Skeleton/Error Inventory | UX | BigDev" (a UX deliverable) while epics line 564 + 687 + UX spec line 109 all name P0-4 as "legal counsel onboarding". Story 0.13 discharges UX/epics's P0-4 (legal counsel onboarding) — NOT architecture's AR-49 line 4783 row. The architecture-side legal-counsel-coverage is in subsidiary rows at architecture lines 4785-4788 (DPDPA grievance officer + FR-43A external forum + Regulatory surface sign-off + Trust formation). Documented as a divergence in `README.md` §10 Domain glossary; flagged for Story 0.15 architectural launch-gate inventory reconciliation; do not patch architecture from a Story.
